A gold standard for access control would be to serve separate models with different capabilities to different users. However, training and deploying multiple models is prohibitively expensive. To address this challenge, we propose gradient-routed auxiliary modules (GRAM), a pre-training method that adds modules to a neural network and selectively updates them to induce specialization. Ablating a module at inference time removes its capability from the network, approximating a model trained on filtered data. We evaluate GRAM on synthetic stories and realistic dual-use data spanning virology, cybersecurity, nuclear physics, and specialized code. These experiments show that GRAM disables targeted capabilities while preserving the rest, and resists their recovery under finetuning better than post-hoc unlearning. Most importantly, a Chinchilla-optimal scaling analysis from 50M to 5B parameters shows that the gap between data-filtered and full-data models widens with scale on removed capabilities but stays small on retained ones, and that GRAM closely tracks data filtering. GRAM's training cost is independent of the number of supported capability profiles, yielding a 5x reduction over data filtering in our 5-profile setting.

中文摘要翻译: AI开发者面临一个双用困境：一项帮助用户治愈疾病的AI能力，同样可以被用来合成疾病。这一困境可以通过访问控制来解决，即将双用AI能力限制在具有合法需求的受信部署中。访问控制的黄金标准是为不同用户提供具备不同能力的独立模型。然而，训练和部署多个模型的成本高得令人望而却步。为应对这一挑战，我们提出了梯度路由辅助模块（GRAM），一种在神经网络中添加辅助模块并通过选择性更新来诱导模块专业化的预训练方法。在推理时消融某一模块即可从网络中移除其对应的能力，近似于在过滤数据上训练的模型。我们在合成故事以及涵盖病毒学、网络安全、核物理和专用代码的真实双用数据上评估了GRAM。实验表明，GRAM能在禁用目标能力的同时保留其他能力，并且在抵抗微调恢复方面优于事后遗忘。最重要的是，从50M到5B参数的Chinchilla最优缩放分析显示，数据过滤模型与全数据模型在移除能力上的差距随规模扩大而增大，而在保留能力上差距保持很小，且GRAM紧密追踪数据过滤的效果。GRAM的训练成本与所支持的能力配置文件数量无关，在我们的5配置文件设置中，相比数据过滤实现了5倍的成本降低。

</details>


### [120] [Deep Learning Method for Stationary Distribution of Reflected Brownian Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08091)
*Jim Dai,Zhanhao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度学习的框架，利用基本伴随关系（BAR）高效学习高维反射布朗运动（RBM）的拉普拉斯变换，并在高维场景下实现了近乎完美的尾概率预测。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 高维随机系统中反射布朗运动的平稳分布和尾概率等关键性能指标难以求解，闭式解仅适用于少数特例，亟需一种通用的计算方法。

Method: 基于基本伴随关系（BAR）设计损失函数、训练数据采样流程和神经网络架构，通过深度学习框架学习高维RBM的拉普拉斯变换。

Result: 在具有已知真实尾概率的RBM实例上评估，该方法在高维设置下实现了近乎完美的预测性能。

Conclusion: 该方法具有作为分析超解析可处理范围外的随机系统的通用工具的潜力。

Original Abstract: The stationary distribution of reflected Brownian motion (RBM) plays an important role in the analysis of high-dimensional stochastic systems, yet closed-form solutions are known only for a few special cases. Computing important performance metrics, such as tail probabilities, is even more intractable, despite their practical relevance. In this paper, we develop a deep learning approach that accurately and efficiently learns the Laplace transform of high-dimensional RBMs based on the basic adjoint relationship (BAR). Our framework combines a careful design of the loss function, training data sampling procedure, and neural network architecture. We evaluate the proposed method on RBM instances with known ground-truth tail probabilities and demonstrate near-perfect prediction in high-dimensional settings, highlighting its potential as a general tool for analyzing stochastic systems beyond analytically tractable regimes. Our code can be found at https://github.com/zhangz73/NN4MGF.

中文摘要翻译: 反射布朗运动（RBM）的平稳分布在高维随机系统分析中扮演着重要角色，然而其闭式解仅已知少数特例。尽管尾概率等关键性能指标具有实际重要性，但其计算更为困难。本文提出一种深度学习方法，基于基本伴随关系（BAR）准确且高效地学习高维RBM的拉普拉斯变换。我们的框架结合了精心设计的损失函数、训练数据采样流程和神经网络架构。我们在具有已知真实尾概率的RBM实例上评估了所提方法，并展示了在高维设置下的近乎完美预测，凸显了其作为分析超解析可处理范围外的随机系统的通用工具的潜力。我们的代码可在https://github.com/zhangz73/NN4MGF获取。

</details>


### [121] [Stochastic Order Learning: An Approach to Rank Estimation Using Noisy Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08103)
*Chaewon Lee,Seon-Ho Lee,Chang-Su Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出随机顺序学习（SOL）框架，将带噪声序数标签的排序估计重新定义为随机排序问题，通过判别损失和随机顺序损失两个互补目标学习嵌入空间，实现可靠排序估计。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 序数标注常呈现结构化不确定性而非简单标签损坏，现有方法难以处理这种噪声下的排序估计问题。

Method: 提出随机顺序学习（SOL）框架，将排序估计重构为随机排序问题，通过判别损失（结构实例-质心交互）和随机顺序损失（施加实例间概率排序关系）两个互补目标学习嵌入空间。

Result: 在多种数据集上的大量实验表明，SOL能够在各种类型和水平的标签噪声下实现可靠的排序估计。

Conclusion: SOL框架有效解决了标签噪声下的序数排序估计问题，通过捕捉序数标签不确定性提升了排序可靠性。

Original Abstract: Rank estimation under label noise poses a fundamental challenge, as ordinal annotations often exhibit structured uncertainty rather than simple label corruption. In this paper, we reformulate rank estimation with noisy ordinal labels as a stochastic ordering problem, in which each instance is inherently associated with multiple plausible ranks instead of a single deterministic label. Based on this view, we propose stochastic order learning (SOL), a learning framework that captures ordinal label uncertainty and learns an embedding space through two complementary objectives: a discriminative loss that structures instance--centroid interactions and a stochastic order loss that enforces probabilistic ordering relations between instances. Extensive experiments across diverse datasets demonstrate that SOL enables reliable rank estimation under various types and levels of label noise. The source code is available at https://github.com/cwlee00/SOL.

中文摘要翻译: 带噪声标签的排序估计是一项基本挑战，因为序数标注通常表现出结构化不确定性，而非简单的标签损坏。在本文中，我们将带噪声序数标签的排序估计重新定义为随机排序问题，其中每个实例本质上关联多个合理的排序而非单个确定性标签。基于这一观点，我们提出了随机顺序学习（SOL），一种通过两个互补目标捕捉序数标签不确定性并学习嵌入空间的框架：一个判别损失用于结构化实例-质心交互，一个随机顺序损失用于施加实例间的概率排序关系。跨多种数据集的大量实验表明，SOL能够在各种类型和水平的标签噪声下实现可靠的排序估计。源代码可在 https://github.com/cwlee00/SOL 获取。

</details>


### [122] [Vanilla SGD with Momentum Survives Heavy-Tailed Noise: Convergence Analysis without Gradient Clipping or Normalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08104)
*Ryusei Yamada,Naoki Sato,Hideaki Iiduka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了普通SGD及其带动量版本在重尾噪声下的收敛性能，证明了其收敛率劣于使用梯度裁剪或归一化的SGD变体，揭示了普通方法在重尾噪声下的固有限制。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 探究一个更基础的问题：普通的SGD（特别是带动量的SGD）在重尾噪声下究竟表现如何？此前相关工作通常通过梯度裁剪或归一化等专门修改来处理重尾噪声，而本文希望在没有这些梯度控制机制的情况下分析普通SGD的表现。

Method: 对普通SGD及带动量的SGD在强凸、凸和非凸目标函数下进行收敛性分析，不采用任何梯度控制机制（如裁剪或归一化），给出首个全面的收敛性分析结果。

Result: 普通SGD及其动量变体在重尾噪声下的收敛率劣于使用梯度裁剪或归一化的SGD变体所达到的最优收敛率，并在合成函数上通过实验验证了理论发现。

Conclusion: 普通SGD及其动量变体在重尾噪声下存在固有限制，其收敛性能不如经过裁剪或归一化处理的SGD变体，揭示了使用梯度控制机制的必要性。

Original Abstract: Stochastic gradient descent (SGD) is a cornerstone of modern optimization. While its performance under heavy-tailed noise is often addressed through specialized modifications such as gradient clipping or normalization, we investigate a more fundamental question: how does vanilla SGD, particularly with momentum, perform in the presence of heavy-tailed noise? In this paper, we refine existing convergence results for vanilla SGD and, more importantly, provide the first comprehensive convergence analysis of vanilla SGD with momentum for strongly convex, convex, and nonconvex objectives, without employing any gradient control mechanisms. Our results demonstrate that the obtained convergence rates are inferior to the optimal rates achieved by clipped or normalized variants of SGD, thereby revealing inherent limitations of vanilla methods under heavy-tailed noise. The theoretical findings are supported by experiments on synthetic functions.

中文摘要翻译: 随机梯度下降（SGD）是现代优化的基石。虽然其在重尾噪声下的性能通常通过梯度裁剪或归一化等专门修改来加以处理，但我们探究了一个更基础的问题：普通SGD，尤其是带动量的版本，在重尾噪声下的表现如何？在本文中，我们细化了普通SGD现有的收敛结果，更重要的是，首次为强凸、凸和非凸目标函数下的带动量普通SGD提供了全面的收敛性分析，且未采用任何梯度控制机制。我们的结果表明，所获得的收敛率劣于经过裁剪或归一化的SGD变体所达到的最优收敛率，从而揭示了普通方法在重尾噪声下的固有限制。理论发现得到了合成函数上实验的支持。

</details>


### [123] [Contrastive Order Learning: A General Framework for Ordinal Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08109)
*Chaewon Lee,BeomJun Shim,Kwang Pyo Choi,Chang-Su Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ConOrd提出了一种对比排序学习框架，通过引入基于排序差异的软亲和力和差异权重的对比排序损失，在批次内所有样本对之间建模细粒度的序数关系，在面部年龄估计、图像/视频质量评估等任务上取得最优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 对比学习虽然能有效利用批次内所有样本，但忽略了标签间的固有排序性；而排序学习显式建模标签序数关系，却往往依赖局部的基于间隔的比较，难以捕获全局序数结构。ConOrd旨在弥补两者的不足。

Method: 提出对比排序学习（ConOrd）框架，核心是引入带软亲和力和差异权重的对比排序损失，权重基于排序差异计算，从而在批次内所有样本对之间实现细粒度的序数关系建模。

Result: 在面部年龄估计、盲图像质量评估、盲视频质量评估等多个序数回归任务上，ConOrd一致取得最先进性能，并展现出良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: ConOrd通过结合对比学习和排序学习的优势，有效克服了各自缺陷，为序数回归提供了一种统一且高效的解决方案。

Original Abstract: We propose contrastive order learning (ConOrd), a contrastive learning framework for ordinal regression that integrates the strengths of contrastive learning and order learning. While contrastive learning effectively leverages all samples in a batch, it typically ignores the inherent ordering among rank labels. Conversely, order learning explicitly models label ordinality but often relies on local, margin-based comparisons, limiting its ability to capture global ordinal structure. ConOrd addresses these limitations by introducing a contrastive order loss with soft affinity and disparity weights based on rank differences, enabling fine-grained modeling of ordinal relationships across all sample pairs within a batch. Extensive experiments on a range of ordinal regression tasks, including facial age estimation, blind image quality assessment, and blind video quality assessment, demonstrate that ConOrd consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance and generalizes well across diverse ordinal regression scenarios. The source code is available at https://github.com/cwlee00/ConOrd.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了对比排序学习（ConOrd），一种用于序数回归的对比学习框架，融合了对比学习和排序学习的优势。对比学习虽然能有效利用批次内的所有样本，但通常忽略了排序标签之间的固有顺序。相反，排序学习显式建模标签的序数性，但往往依赖局部的基于间隔的比较，限制了其捕获全局序数结构的能力。ConOrd通过引入一种带有软亲和力和差异权重的对比排序损失来克服这些局限性，其中权重基于排序差异计算，从而在批次内的所有样本对之间实现细粒度的序数关系建模。在包括面部年龄估计、盲图像质量评估和盲视频质量评估等一系列序数回归任务上的大量实验表明，ConOrd一致取得了最先进的性能，并在多种序数回归场景中展现出良好的泛化能力。源代码可在 https://github.com/cwlee00/ConOrd 获取。

</details>


### [124] [Workload-Preserving Differentially Private Synthetic Data for Causal Inference via Maximum-Entropy Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08122)
*Amir Asiaee,Kaveh Aryan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“因果工作负载”（causal workloads），一种专为平均处理效应（ATE）等因果估计量设计的差分隐私查询集，通过正交矩匹配与最大熵校准生成可复用的合成数据，并引入Causal-AIM自适应选择器与噪声感知多重插补（NA+MI）置信区间方法。实验表明，因果工作负载在严格隐私预算下优于通用方法，而通用方法在宽松隐私下点RMSE更优，揭示了分布保真度与因果推断有效性之间的权衡。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于工作负载的差分隐私合成数据方法主要针对通用查询集设计，能实现良好的分布保真度，但因果估计量（如ATE）依赖于处理组平衡和结果矩信息，通用边际查询无法有效保留这些因果矩，导致因果推断偏差。

Method: 1）设计因果工作负载：围绕双稳健因果估计器所使用的正交矩构造差分隐私查询集；2）提出Causal-AIM自适应工作负载选择器；3）提出噪声感知多重插补（NA+MI）程序用于合成数据的置信区间估计；4）通过最大熵校准将查询结果重建为可复用合成数据。

Result: 理论层面将ATE误差分解为采样、隐私、工作负载近似、蒙特卡洛和校准五个分量。实验表明因果工作负载在严格隐私预算下最有效，且能支持ATE、ATT和子组分析而不消耗额外隐私预算；通用工作负载在隐私宽松时点RMSE更优。

Conclusion: 分布保真度有助于点估计精度，但有效的因果推断必须保留因果矩并将DP噪声纳入推断过程，而非将合成行视为真实数据。因果工作负载在严格隐私下优势明显，揭示了隐私保护因果推断中关键的设计权衡。

Original Abstract: Workload-based differentially private (DP) synthetic data methods privately measure aggregate queries and post-process the noisy answers into synthetic records. Generic workloads can achieve strong distributional fidelity, but causal estimands such as the average treatment effect (ATE) depend on treatment-arm balance and outcome moments that generic marginals need not preserve. We propose causal workloads: DP query sets designed around the orthogonal moments used by doubly robust causal estimators. The released workload can be used directly by stable moment-map estimators or reconstructed by maximum-entropy calibration into reusable synthetic data; our theory decomposes ATE error into sampling, privacy, workload-approximation, Monte Carlo, and calibration terms. We also introduce Causal-AIM, an adaptive workload selector, and a noise-aware multiple-imputation (NA+MI) procedure for confidence intervals from DP synthetic data. Because the workload is released once, the same DP synthetic table can support ATE, ATT, and subgroup analyses without additional privacy spending. Empirically, causal workloads are most useful at strict privacy budgets and for calibrated uncertainty, while generic workloads often retain an advantage for point RMSE as privacy relaxes. The broader lesson is a tradeoff: distributional fidelity can help point accuracy, but valid causal inference requires preserving causal moments and propagating DP noise rather than treating synthetic rows as real.

中文摘要翻译: 基于工作负载的差分隐私（DP）合成数据方法通过私下聚合查询并后处理含噪答案来生成合成记录。通用工作负载能够实现良好的分布保真度，但平均处理效应（ATE）等因果估计量依赖于处理组平衡和结果矩信息，通用边际查询未必能保留这些信息。本文提出因果工作负载：围绕双稳健因果估计器所使用的正交矩设计的差分隐私查询集。释放的工作负载可直接用于稳定的矩映射估计器，或通过最大熵校准重建为可复用的合成数据；我们的理论将ATE误差分解为采样、隐私、工作负载近似、蒙特卡洛和校准五个分量。我们还引入了Causal-AIM——一种自适应工作负载选择器，以及用于从差分隐私合成数据中构建置信区间的噪声感知多重插补（NA+MI）程序。由于工作负载仅释放一次，同一张DP合成表可以支持ATE、ATT和子组分析，而无需额外消耗隐私预算。实验表明，因果工作负载在严格隐私预算下以及需要校准不确定性时最为有效，而通用工作负载在隐私宽松时通常在点RMSE上保持优势。更广泛的启示是一对权衡：分布保真度有助于点估计精度，但有效的因果推断需要保留因果矩并传播DP噪声，而不是将合成行视为真实数据。

</details>


### [125] [DeepPySR -- A Symbolic Regression Framework with Dynamic Pruning, Pareto Selection, and Hierarchical Composition for Real-World Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08150)
*Fuling Chen,Kevin Vinsen,Phillip Melton,Rae-Chi Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DeepPySR提出了一种深度符号回归方法，通过动态变量剪枝、指数帕累托选择和多层层次化符号组合，有效解决了高维输入、帕累托前沿选择和数据不规则性三大挑战，在物理、生物医学和社会科学数据集上均优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 符号回归能生成可解释的白箱模型，但现有方法面临高维输入、帕累托前沿公式的原则性选择以及多重共线性、类别不平衡等数据不规则性问题，限制了其在临床医学和社会科学等关键领域的应用。

Method: 提出DeepPySR方法，包含三个核心组件：(1) 动态变量剪枝调度，在搜索过程中逐步移除无关特征；(2) 指数帕累托选择准则，消除准确性与复杂度之间的权衡；(3) 多层架构实现层次化符号组合。

Result: 在四个费曼物理基准和七个生物医学/社会科学数据集上，DeepPySR全面超越PySR等基线方法：体脂R² 0.794 vs 0.702，心脏病F1 0.898 vs 0.787，学生成绩R² 0.964 vs 0.948，Raine BMI R² 0.525 vs 0.370，且生成的可解释公式与领域风险因素一致。

Conclusion: DeepPySR通过创新的变量剪枝、帕累托选择和层次化组合策略，有效解决了符号回归在真实场景中的关键挑战，在多个重要应用领域取得了更优性能和可解释性。

Original Abstract: Symbolic regression (SR) discovers analytical equations from data, yielding glass-box models with directly interpretable formulas, unlike black-box methods that rely on unstable post-hoc tools such as SHAP or LIME. This transparency is crucial in clinical medicine and social science, but SR faces three challenges: high-dimensional inputs, principled selection of Pareto-front formulae, and data irregularities such as multicollinearity and class imbalance. We introduce DeepPySR, which addresses these issues with a dynamic variable-pruning schedule to remove irrelevant features during search, an exponential Pareto selection criterion that eliminates trade-offs between accuracy and complexity, and a multi-layer architecture for hierarchical symbolic composition. On four Feynman physics benchmarks and seven biomedical and social-science datasets, DeepPySR outperforms PySR and baselines on body fat (R$^2$: 0.794 vs.\ 0.702), heart disease (F1: 0.898 vs.\ 0.787), student performance (R$^2$: 0.964 vs.\ 0.948), and Raine BMI (R$^2$: 0.525 vs.\ 0.370), producing interpretable formulas aligned with domain risk factors.

中文摘要翻译: 符号回归（SR）能够从数据中发现解析方程，生成具有直接可解释公式的白箱模型，而非依赖SHAP或LIME等不稳定事后工具的黑箱方法。这种透明性在临床医学和社会科学中至关重要，但符号回归面临三大挑战：高维输入、帕累托前沿公式的原则性选择，以及多重共线性和类别不平衡等数据不规则性。我们提出了DeepPySR，通过动态变量剪枝调度在搜索过程中移除无关特征、采用消除准确性与复杂度之间权衡的指数帕累托选择准则，以及用于层次化符号组合的多层架构来解决这些问题。在四个费曼物理基准和七个生物医学与社会科学数据集上，DeepPySR在体脂（R²: 0.794 vs. 0.702）、心脏病（F1: 0.898 vs. 0.787）、学生成绩（R²: 0.964 vs. 0.948）和Raine BMI（R²: 0.525 vs. 0.370）等任务中均优于PySR及其他基线方法，生成了与领域风险因素一致的可解释公式。

</details>


### [126] [Understanding Layer Patching in Model Size Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08170)
*Sara Kangaslahti,Jonathan Geuter,Nihal V. Nayak,Marco Fumero,Francesco Locatello,David Alvarez-Melis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了零样本模型尺寸插值中的学生层选择问题，将最优层子集选择转化为最短路径优化问题，并提出基于KL散度的贪心算法KLPatch，实验表明简单顺序策略效果意外地好，而KLPatch可进一步逼近最优解。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有boomerang蒸馏方法通过迭代修补学生层来获得中间尺寸模型，但缺乏对学生层选择策略的系统性研究，不同选择策略对插值行为的影响尚不清楚。

Method: 将最优层子集选择建模为有向无环图中的最短路径优化问题；提出基于KL散度的贪心修补算法KLPatch；在多个模型家族上系统比较不同修补策略（从头到尾、从尾到头、KLPatch等）。

Result: 1）修补策略显著影响插值行为且效果因模型家族而异；2）简单的顺序策略（从头到尾或从尾到头）在实践中表现惊人地好；3）KLPatch算法通常优于从尾到头修补，并能近似求解优化问题。

Conclusion: 为层修补如何影响模型尺寸插值提供了原理性理解，为构建接近最优的插值模型提供了实用指导。

Original Abstract: Zero-shot model size interpolation aims to create new models of intermediate target sizes by combining existing models without additional training. Recent work on boomerang distillation [Kangaslahti et al., 2026] shows that a student language model distilled from a larger teacher can be expanded by iteratively patching its layers, replacing student layers with contiguous blocks of teacher layers to obtain models whose size and performance interpolate between the student and the teacher. In this work, we provide the first systematic study of student-layer selection for model size interpolation. We cast finding the optimal layer subset for each model size as an optimization problem and prove it can be viewed as a shortest-path problem in a certain acyclic graph. In experiments, we show that patching strongly shapes interpolation behavior, with effects that vary substantially across model families. We find that simple sequential strategies--patching either from the first layer to the last or from the last to the first--often achieve surprisingly strong performance in practice. We further introduce KLPatch, a greedy patching algorithm based on KL divergence, which often improves over last-to-first patching and approximately solves the optimization problem. Together, our results provide a principled understanding of how layer patching affects model size interpolation and offer practical guidance for constructing near-optimal interpolated models.

中文摘要翻译: 零样本模型尺寸插值旨在通过组合现有模型来创建中间目标尺寸的新模型，无需额外训练。近期关于boomerang蒸馏的工作[Kangaslahti et al., 2026]表明，从较大教师模型蒸馏得到的学生语言模型可以通过迭代修补其层来扩展，即用教师层的连续块替换学生层，从而获得尺寸和性能介于学生和教师之间的模型。在这项工作中，我们首次对模型尺寸插值中的学生层选择进行了系统性研究。我们将为每个模型尺寸寻找最优层子集的问题形式化为一个优化问题，并证明该问题可视为某个有向无环图中的最短路径问题。实验中，我们展示了修补策略显著影响插值行为，且其效果在不同模型家族间差异很大。我们发现简单的顺序策略——即从头到尾或从尾到头进行修补——在实践中往往能达到令人惊讶的强性能。我们进一步提出了KLPatch，一种基于KL散度的贪心修补算法，该算法通常优于从尾到头修补，并能近似求解上述优化问题。综合来看，我们的研究结果为理解层修补如何影响模型尺寸插值提供了原理性基础，并为构建接近最优的插值模型提供了实用指导。

</details>


### [127] [Open-ended Multi-agent Autocurricula via Visual Inspection of Policies with Multi-modal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08193)
*Lorenzo Pantè,Andrea Fanti,Roberto Capobianco*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VIP方法，利用视频语言模型（VLM）直接观察智能体的策略视频来评估任务难度并生成课程推荐，在SMAC环境中取得了优于纯文本方法和标量任务分数方法的效果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有开放课程学习方法主要依赖标量任务分数或文本摘要来评估任务难度，但这些方式可能丢失策略行为的丰富信息。作者探索了一种不同思路：直接通过录制的策略视频来评估智能体行为，从而更准确地判断任务难度并设计课程。

Method: 提出VIP（Visual Inspection of Policies）方法，使用轻量级且开放的VLM（VideoLLaMa2-7B）处理策略录制的视频，直接观察智能体行为，基于视频分析结果生成课程推荐。

Result: 在StarCraft Multi-Agent Challenge（SMAC）环境中，VIP使用策略视频生成的课程优于纯文本消融方法和依赖标量任务分数的方法。

Conclusion: 直接利用视频语言模型分析策略行为视频是一种有效的课程学习方式，即使使用轻量级VLM也能取得比文本和标量方法更好的效果，为开放课程学习提供了新方向。

Original Abstract: Open-ended curricula in Reinforcement Learning (RL) aim to train generally-capable agents by identifying tasks that facilitate learning increasingly complex skills. A major challenge when designing such curricula is assessing task difficulty relative to the agent's current learning progress. While previous work has explored using scalar task scores or textual summaries of the agent's behavior, here we study a different approach: directly inspecting policy behavior via recorded episode videos. We introduce a simple yet effective instantiation of this approach which leverages a Video Language Model (VLM) to both process these videos and provide curriculum recommendations, which we call Visual Inspection of Policies (VIP). Since videos can naturally contain any number of controllable agents, we empirically study VIP on the StarCraft Multi-Agent Challenge (SMAC). We show that even with a lightweight and openly accessible VLM (VideoLLaMa2-7B), VIP can use policy videos to generate more effective curricula than both its text-only ablation and methods that rely on scalar task scores.

中文摘要翻译: 强化学习中的开放课程旨在通过识别促进日益复杂技能学习的任务，来训练具有通用能力的智能体。设计此类课程的一个主要挑战是评估任务相对于智能体当前学习进展的难度。虽然先前的工作探索了使用标量任务分数或智能体行为的文本摘要，但本文研究了一种不同的方法：通过录制的回合视频直接检查策略行为。我们引入了一种简单而有效的该方法的实例化，它利用视频语言模型（VLM）同时处理这些视频并提供课程推荐，称为策略的视觉检查（VIP）。由于视频自然可以包含任意数量的可控智能体，我们实证研究了VIP在星际争霸多智能体挑战（SMAC）上的表现。我们表明，即使使用轻量级且开放的VLM（VideoLLaMa2-7B），VIP也可以利用策略视频生成比纯文本消融和依赖标量任务分数的方法更有效的课程。

</details>


### [128] [PIT-SUN: A Deployable Empirical Marginal Transform Framework with Expectation-Consistent Recovery for Regression in Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08202)
*Mingyu Zhao,Zhaohan Li,Zhenxiong Miao,Xu Zhang,Dewei Leng,Yanan Niu,Kun Gai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PIT-SUN框架，通过概率积分变换和乘法SUN恢复，在不直接逆变换的情况下实现原始空间条件期望的无偏估计，解决了重尾、零膨胀和多模态目标下的期望一致性问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 标准MSE损失在重尾、零膨胀和多模态目标上梯度不稳定，导致均值坍缩和尾部收缩。非线性边际变换虽能缓解尺度冲突，但直接逆变换无法保证期望一致性（仅有仿射逆变换能做到），而已有的条件线性恢复方法对稀疏复杂边际的坐标选取、逆查找、恢复基和部署监控等环节缺乏明确方案。

Method: 提出PIT-SUN框架，包含四个组件：1) 基于经验边际表定义有界正态分数坐标（概率积分变换）；2) 逆分位数查找；3) 方差控制的恢复基；4) 漂移监控。最终使用乘法随机无偏归一化（SUN）恢复来估计原始空间期望，而非直接逆变换变换后的预测。

Result: 在合成分布、公开基准、大规模工业数据集和在线部署上的实验表明，PIT-SUN在点估计精度、校准性和排序质量上均有稳健提升，且部署开销低。

Conclusion: PIT-SUN是一个可部署的经验边际恢复框架，有效解决了价值驱动推荐系统中条件期望估计的期望一致性问题，兼顾了理论保证与工程实用性。

Original Abstract: Estimating original-space conditional expectations is central to value-driven recommender systems, including dwell time, GMV, and LTV forecasting. Standard MSE is expectation-consistent in principle, but its gradients become unstable on heavy-tailed, zero-inflated, and multimodal targets, causing mean collapse and tail shrinkage. Target transformation alleviates this scale conflict, yet any useful nonlinear marginal transform loses expectation consistency under direct inversion. This is not an implementation oversight: a direct inverse-transform estimator is universally expectation-consistent only when the inverse transform is affine, which cannot simultaneously provide bounded tail compression. Existing conditionally linear recovery methods restore expectation consistency, but still leave open which coordinate, inverse lookup, recovery base, and deployment monitor should be selected for sparse complex marginals. We propose \textbf{P}robability-\textbf{I}ntegral-\textbf{TranS}formed \textbf{Un}biased recovery (\textbf{PIT-SUN}), a deployable empirical marginal recovery framework. PIT-SUN uses one empirical marginal table to define a bounded normal-score coordinate, its inverse-quantile lookup, a variance-controlled recovery base, and drift monitoring, then applies multiplicative SUN recovery to estimate the original-space expectation instead of directly inverting transformed predictions. Experiments on synthetic distributions, public benchmarks, large-scale industrial datasets, and online deployment show robust improvements in point accuracy, calibration, and ranking quality with lightweight deployment overhead.

中文摘要翻译: 估计原始空间的条件期望是价值驱动推荐系统的核心，包括驻留时间、GMV和LTV预测。标准MSE在原则上具有期望一致性，但在重尾、零膨胀和多模态目标上梯度不稳定，导致均值坍缩和尾部收缩。目标变换缓解了这种尺度冲突，但任何有用的非线性边际变换在直接逆变换下都会失去期望一致性。这并非实现疏忽：直接逆变换估计器仅当逆变换为仿射变换时才能普遍保持期望一致性，而仿射变换无法同时提供有界的尾部压缩。现有的条件线性恢复方法恢复了期望一致性，但对于稀疏复杂边际，在坐标选择、逆查找、恢复基和部署监控等方面仍有待明确。我们提出概率积分变换无偏恢复（PIT-SUN），一个可部署的经验边际恢复框架。PIT-SUN使用一张经验边际表来定义有界正态分数坐标、其逆分位数查找、方差控制的恢复基和漂移监控，然后应用乘法SUN恢复来估计原始空间期望，而非直接逆变换变换后的预测。在合成分布、公开基准、大规模工业数据集和在线部署上的实验表明，PIT-SUN在点估计精度、校准性和排序质量上均有稳健提升，且部署开销低。

</details>


### [129] [RhyMix: A Lightweight Adaptive Multi-Rhythm Network for Long-Term Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08234)
*Sumit Satishrao Shevtekar,Chandresh Kumar Maurya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RhyMix是一种轻量级混合神经网络架构，通过并行双路径建模（循环路径+多尺度时序卷积路径）和自适应门控机制，在12个真实数据集中的10个上实现长期时序预测SOTA，参数量仅~40K，推理延迟<5ms。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有时序预测架构（Transformer、CNN、线性模型）采用单路径时序建模，无法同时捕捉短期波动、周期循环、长期趋势和突变的复杂动态特征，各自存在局限性（Transformer平滑局部变化、CNN感受野有限、线性模型无法建模非线性）。

Method: 提出RhyMix混合架构，包含两条互补编码分支：(i) 循环路径（Cyclic Path），通过可学习循环嵌入引入显式季节性归纳偏置；(ii) 轻量级多尺度时序卷积网络+通道注意力路径（MSTCN-CA Path），采用多尺度深度可分离膨胀卷积捕捉不同感受野的时序依赖。关键创新是多层次自适应门控：路径门动态组合四个预测头（直接、趋势-季节分解、局部卷积、周期融合），混合门根据输入特征自适应平衡两条路径的输出。

Result: 在12个真实数据集的长期预测基准上，RhyMix在10个数据集达到SOTA；模型轻量（~40K参数），具有线性复杂度，推理延迟低（<5ms），适用于资源受限的边缘设备和实时部署。

Conclusion: RhyMix通过并行双路径建模和自适应门控机制，有效解决了单路径时序建模的局限性，在保持线性复杂度和极低参数量的同时实现了SOTA预测性能，适合边缘端实时部署。

Original Abstract: Real-world time series exhibit complex dynamics characterized by multiple simultaneous temporal patterns: short-term fluctuations, periodic seasonal cycles, long-term trends, and irregular abrupt changes. However, many existing forecasting architectures rely on single-path temporal modeling--transformers capture long-range dependencies but smooth local variations, convolutions capture local patterns but have limited receptive fields, and linear models are efficient but cannot capture nonlinear dynamics. To address this, we introduce RhyMix (RHYthm MIXture), a hybrid neural architecture designed around a parallel dual-path modeling paradigm with adaptive gating mechanisms. RhyMix integrates two complementary encoding branches: (i) a Cyclic Path that incorporates explicit seasonal inductive bias through learnable cyclic embeddings, capturing predictable rhythmic patterns; and (ii) a lightweight Multi-Scale Temporal Convolutional Network with Channel Attention Path that employs multi-scale depthwise dilated convolutions to capture temporal dependencies across different receptive fields. A key innovation is the use of adaptive gating at multiple levels: a path gate dynamically combines four specialized forecasting heads (Direct, Trend-Seasonal Decomposition, Local Convolution, and Periodic Fusion) per sample and channel, while a hybrid gate adaptively balances the Cyclic and MSTCN-CA Paths based on input characteristics. This design ensures the model adapts to specific temporal patterns while maintaining linear complexity in sequence length, channels, and prediction horizon. Across extensive benchmarks on 12 real-world datasets for long-term forecasting, RhyMix achieves state-of-the-art performance on 10 of 12 datasets. The model remains lightweight (~40K params) with linear complexity and low-latency inference (<5ms),suitable for resource-constrained edge devices and real-time deployment.

中文摘要翻译: 真实世界的时间序列呈现出由多种同时发生的时间模式所刻画的复杂动态：短期波动、周期性季节循环、长期趋势以及不规则突变。然而，许多现有的预测架构依赖于单路径时序建模——Transformer能够捕获长程依赖但会平滑局部变化，卷积可以捕获局部模式但感受野有限，线性模型虽然高效却无法捕捉非线性动态。为解决这一问题，我们提出了RhyMix（RHYthm MIXture，节奏混合），一种围绕并行双路径建模范式设计的混合神经网络架构，并配备了自适应门控机制。RhyMix集成了两条互补的编码分支：(i) 循环路径（Cyclic Path），通过可学习的循环嵌入引入显式的季节性归纳偏置，捕获可预测的节奏模式；(ii) 轻量级多尺度时序卷积网络与通道注意力路径（Multi-Scale Temporal Convolutional Network with Channel Attention Path），采用多尺度深度可分离膨胀卷积来捕捉不同感受野下的时序依赖关系。一个关键的创新是多层次自适应门控的使用：路径门（path gate）为每个样本和通道动态组合四个专门的预测头（直接预测头、趋势-季节分解头、局部卷积头和周期融合头），而混合门（hybrid gate）则根据输入特征自适应地平衡循环路径和MSTCN-CA路径。这种设计确保了模型能够适应特定的时间模式，同时在序列长度、通道数和预测范围上保持线性复杂度。在12个真实数据集的长期预测广泛基准测试中，RhyMix在10个数据集上达到了最先进的性能。该模型保持轻量化（约40K参数），具有线性复杂度和低延迟推理（<5ms），适用于资源受限的边缘设备和实时部署场景。

</details>


### [130] [Structure Learning on Clustered Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08238)
*Ryan Thompson,Matt P. Wand,Veerabhadran Baladandayuthapani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向聚类数据的DAG结构学习方法，通过扩展混合模型中的固定与随机效应框架，引入可微图耦合机制保证无环性，并设计了收敛性有保证的一阶优化方法，在理论和实验上证明了模型的可辨识性与渐近一致性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有DAG结构学习方法假设总体完全同质，无法处理存在聚类特定变异（如患者特定效应）的聚类数据，亟需一种能同时估计全局结构并考虑局部聚类效应的方法。

Method: 将经典混合模型中的固定效应与随机效应框架扩展到结构学习场景，提出可微图耦合机制，确保固定效应图与随机效应图的并集保持无环；采用可证明收敛的一阶优化方法，并利用跨聚类的批量更新提高效率。

Result: 在真实和合成数据实验中，该方法能够检测到其他替代估计器遗漏的依赖关系；理论上证明了模型可辨识性及渐近恢复真实结构的能力。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效弥补了现有DAG结构学习在聚类数据场景下的空白，兼顾全局结构与局部变异，具有理论保证和实际性能优势。

Original Abstract: Recent algorithmic advances have made directed acyclic graph (DAG) structure learning scalable for causal discovery. Yet, the currently available techniques assume a completely homogeneous population, precluding their application to clustered data where cluster-specific variations (e.g., patient-specific effects) are common. We address this issue by introducing a new approach that estimates a global structure while accounting for local cluster-level effects. The key idea is to extend the fixed- and random-effects framework of classical mixed models to the structure learning setting. Towards this end, we present a differentiable graph coupling mechanism that guarantees the union of the fixed- and random-effects graphs remains acyclic. Computationally, we provide a provably convergent first-order method and leverage efficient batched updates across clusters. Statistically, we establish identifiability of the model and show that our approach recovers the true structure asymptotically. In experiments on real and synthetic data, our proposal detects dependencies missed by alternative estimators, underscoring its value for structure learning in clustered settings.

中文摘要翻译: 近期的算法进展使得有向无环图（DAG）结构学习在因果发现中具备了可扩展性。然而，现有技术假设总体完全同质，这阻碍了它们应用于聚类数据——其中聚类特定的变异（例如患者特定效应）是常见的。我们通过引入一种新方法来解决这个问题，该方法在估计全局结构的同时考虑了局部的聚类级效应。关键思想是将经典混合模型中的固定效应和随机效应框架扩展到结构学习场景。为此，我们提出一种可微图耦合机制，确保固定效应图与随机效应图的并集保持无环性。在计算层面，我们提供了一种可证明收敛的一阶方法，并利用跨聚类的批量更新来提高效率。在统计层面，我们建立了模型的可辨识性，并证明我们的方法能够渐近恢复真实结构。在真实数据和合成数据上的实验中，我们的方法检测到了其他替代估计器遗漏的依赖关系，突显了其在聚类场景下进行结构学习的价值。

</details>


### [131] [An interpretable Good--Turing restart criterion for k-means++](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08243)
*Renato Cordeiro de Amorim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GTRC准则，基于Good-Turing估计和置信度边界动态决定k-means++算法是否需要重启，替代了固定重启次数的做法，在36个数据集上验证了其有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: k-means++算法通常需要多次重启以避免局部最优，但重启次数几乎总是任意选择且对所有数据集统一使用，这既浪费计算资源（对简单数据集）又可能不够（对困难数据集），且影响基于此的实验比较的公平性。

Method: 提出GTRC（Good-Turing Restart Criterion）重启准则，它结合了Good-Turing估计、一个经证明的无条件界以及一个基于置信度的界，用于估计再次重启能否改进当前结果的概率，当该概率低于用户指定的容忍度ε时停止重启。

Result: 在36个数据集上，GTRC达到了与精心选择的固定重启次数相当的聚类质量，但重启次数随数据集难度自适应变化，受可解释的、数据依赖的信号而非固定规则支配。

Conclusion: GTRC为预先固定k-means++重启次数提供了一种有理论依据的、可报告的替代方案。

Original Abstract: The k-means++ algorithm is commonly restarted multiple times to avoid poor local optima, yet the number of restarts is almost always chosen arbitrarily and applied uniformly regardless of data set difficulty. This undermines any comparison relying on such a choice and wastes computation on easy data sets while potentially under-serving hard ones. We introduce GTRC, a restart criterion combining a Good-Turing estimate, a proven unconditional bound, and a confidence-based bound on the probability that a further restart would improve on the current result, stopping once this probability falls below a user-specified tolerance $\varepsilon$. Across 36 data sets, GTRC reached clustering quality competitive with well-chosen fixed restart counts, while the number of restarts used varied considerably and appropriately with data set difficulty, governed by an interpretable, data-dependent signal rather than a fixed rule. GTRC offers a principled and reportable alternative to fixing the number of $k$-means++ restarts in advance. Software:https://github.com/RCdeAmorim/Good-Turing-Restart-Criterion.

中文摘要翻译: k-means++算法通常需要多次重启以避免陷入较差的局部最优解，然而重启次数几乎总是任意选择且无视数据集难度而统一使用。这种做法损害了依赖该选择的任何比较的公平性，在简单数据集上浪费计算资源，同时可能对困难数据集处理不足。我们提出了GTRC（Good-Turing重启准则），该准则结合了Good-Turing估计、一个经过证明的无条件界以及一个基于置信度的界，用于估计再次重启能否改进当前结果的概率，当该概率低于用户指定的容忍度ε时停止重启。在36个数据集上，GTRC达到了与精心选择的固定重启次数相竞争的聚类质量，而所使用的重启次数根据数据集难度发生了显著且适当的变化，其由可解释的、数据依赖的信号而非固定规则所支配。GTRC为预先固定k-means++重启次数提供了一种有理论依据的、可报告的替代方案。软件：https://github.com/RCdeAmorim/Good-Turing-Restart-Criterion。

</details>


### [132] [CASL-VAE: Learning Structured Latent Variables from Unpaired Data for Semi-supervised Clustering and Paired Sample Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08254)
*Sai Spandana Chintapalli,Pratik Chaudhari,Christos Davatzikos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CASL-VAE，一种深度对比潜变量模型，用于从非配对数据中学习结构化潜在生成因子，分离参考群体和目标群体的变异，识别疾病亚型并生成配对样本。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法在没有配对数据且存在异质性目标变异时，难以分离多种目标特异性变异模式，无法有效建模疾病亚型等离散异质性。

Method: CASL-VAE将变异分解为跨群体共享的连续共同潜因子和层级化显著潜因子（离散亚型+连续亚型内变异），通过变分推断利用非配对数据优化参考域和目标域的近似联合似然。

Result: 在半合成神经影像数据上，CASL-VAE在亚型恢复和配对样本生成方面优于基线聚类和生成模型；在真实阿尔茨海默病数据中揭示了生物学合理的异质性。

Conclusion: CASL-VAE为无配对数据下的跨群体变异分解和疾病异质性建模提供了有效方案。

Original Abstract: Quantifying variability in a target population relative to a reference population is central to many scientific and clinical problems (e.g., diseased vs. healthy). Yet, without paired data and in the presence of heterogeneous target variation, existing methods struggle to separate multiple modes of target-specific variation. We propose \textit{CASL-VAE}, a deep contrastive latent variable model that learns structured latent generative factors from unpaired data. CASL-VAE factorizes variation into continuous common latent factors shared across populations and hierarchical salient latent factors that model target-specific heterogeneity as discrete subtypes and continuous within-subtype variation. Using variational inference, we show how approximate joint likelihood optimization over reference and target domains can be performed using unpaired data, providing a principled basis for paired-sample generation and cross-domain analysis. We validate CASL-VAE on semi-synthetic neuroimaging data, demonstrating improved subtype recovery and paired-sample generation compared to baseline clustering and generative models. We also validate its ability to reveal biologically plausible heterogeneity in Alzheimer's disease.

中文摘要翻译: 量化目标群体相对于参考群体的变异性是许多科学和临床问题的核心（例如患病与健康）。然而，在没有配对数据且存在异质性目标变异的情况下，现有方法难以分离多种目标特异性变异模式。我们提出CASL-VAE，一种深度对比潜变量模型，能够从非配对数据中学习结构化的潜在生成因子。CASL-VAE将变异分解为跨群体共享的连续共同潜因子，以及将目标特异性异质性建模为离散亚型和连续亚型内变异的层级化显著潜因子。利用变分推断，我们展示了如何通过非配对数据在参考域和目标域上进行近似联合似然优化，从而为配对样本生成和跨域分析提供理论基础。我们在半合成神经影像数据上验证了CASL-VAE，结果表明其在亚型恢复和配对样本生成方面优于基线聚类和生成模型。我们还在阿尔茨海默病数据中验证了其揭示生物学合理异质性的能力。

</details>


### [133] [Classifier Chain-based Pathological Test Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08299)
*Abu Rafe Md Jamil,Nayan Malakar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Classifier Chain的多标签病理检验推荐系统，利用患者症状在医生会诊前推荐检验项目，结合多种机器学习算法和SHAP可解释性分析，在真实数据集上达到98.83%的准确率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 诊断检验的延迟推荐和医生的主观解读会阻碍有效诊疗，因此需要一种能在医生会诊前基于患者症状加速检验选择过程的自动化推荐系统。

Method: 将检验推荐任务建模为多标签分类问题，采用Classifier Chain（CC）技术捕获检验项目之间的依赖关系；使用Logistic Regression、Decision Tree、Random Forest等机器学习算法进行对比；并利用SHAP进行模型可解释性分析。

Result: Logistic Regression with CC模型总体准确率达98.83%；Majority Voting集成模型在精确率0.93、召回率0.85、F1-score 0.89上取得最佳平衡；SHAP分析显示模型推理与医学知识一致。

Conclusion: Classifier Chain能够提升传统算法在诊断过程中的效率，提供准确的检验推荐，并增强临床可解释性和医生决策信心。

Original Abstract: Accurate and timely diagnoses are essential for quality patient care. However, delayed recommendation of diagnostic tests and physicians' subjective interpretations can hinder effective care. This study introduces a pathological test recommendation system that speeds up the test selection process using patient symptoms before physician consultation. The recommendation task is framed as a multi-label classification problem utilising the Classifier Chain (CC) technique to consider dependencies between tests. We collected data from the SOUTHERN.IML pathology and then created a custom dataset with the help of the expertise. Multiple machine learning algorithms, including Logistic Regression, Decision Tree, and Random Forest, were applied to compare models and identify the best fit for our study context. The Logistic Regression with CC model had the highest overall accuracy at 98.83%, while the Majority Voting ensemble model provided the best balance with a precision of 0.93, recall of 0.85, and F1-score of 0.89. To ensure transparency of the models and clinical interpretability, we used Explainable AI (XAI) techniques utilising SHAP (SHapley Additive Explanations), which identifies how each symptom is contributing to a test recommendation. The diagnostic reasoning revealed by the model was consistent with established medical knowledge of symptoms for the recommended tests, which further adds confidence to the model's reliability for diagnostic purposes. The reasoning could help physicians make logical decisions in critical scenarios. Overall, our findings suggest that CC can improve the efficiency of the traditional algorithms in diagnostic process providing accurate test recommendations.

中文摘要翻译: 准确及时的诊断对于优质患者护理至关重要。然而，诊断检验的延迟推荐以及医生的主观解读可能会阻碍有效诊疗。本研究引入了一种病理检验推荐系统，该系统在医生会诊前利用患者症状加速检验选择过程。该推荐任务被建模为一个多标签分类问题，利用Classifier Chain（CC）技术来考虑检验项目之间的依赖关系。我们从SOUTHERN.IML病理实验室收集数据，并借助领域专家知识构建了自定义数据集。应用了包括逻辑回归、决策树和随机森林在内的多种机器学习算法进行模型比较，以确定最适合本研究场景的模型。采用CC的逻辑回归模型总体准确率最高，达到98.83%；而多数投票集成模型在精确率0.93、召回率0.85和F1-score 0.89上取得了最佳平衡。为保障模型的透明度和临床可解释性，我们使用了基于SHAP（SHapley Additive Explanations）的可解释人工智能（XAI）技术，该技术能够识别每个症状对检验推荐的贡献程度。模型揭示的诊断推理与已建立的、关于推荐检验项目对应症状的医学知识一致，这进一步增强了模型用于诊断目的的可信度。该推理过程能够帮助医生在关键场景中做出理性决策。总体而言，我们的研究表明，Classifier Chain能够提升传统算法在诊断过程中的效率，提供准确的检验推荐。

</details>


### [134] [Learning $\mathsf{AC}^0$ under Locally Sampleable Graphical Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08303)
*Weiming Feng,Xiongxin Yang,Yixiao Yu,Yiyao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个拟多项式时间学习器，用于在具有高效局部采样器的图模型下学习AC⁰电路，通过模拟并截断经典Glauber动力学来建立吉布斯分布的低度近似，从而绕过了先前工作中对多项式增长条件的要求。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: Linial等人提出的低度算法能够在均匀分布下拟多项式时间学习AC⁰电路，但将该结果推广到更广泛的相关分布（如图模型）一直是一个长期挑战。先前工作虽然将保证扩展到满足强空间混合和多项式增长的有界度图模型上的吉布斯分布，但多项式增长条件限制了其应用范围。本文旨在去除这一限制，使得学习器能够适用于更一般的图模型。

Method: 通过模拟并适当截断经典Glauber动力学，建立吉布斯分布的一种新的低度近似，从而设计出拟多项式时间学习器。

Result: 在任意有界度图上，对于二自旋系统（包括硬核模型和伊辛模型），在接近各自采样阈值的参数区域上，该框架都能有效地进行学习。

Conclusion: 本文成功去除了先前工作中的多项式增长要求，将AC⁰电路的学习器推广到仅需高效局部采样器的图模型上，拓展了低度算法的适用范围。

Original Abstract: The problem of learning constant-depth circuits holds profound implications for computational learning theory. In a seminal result, by introducing the low-degree algorithm, Linial, Mansour, and Nisan (J. ACM 1993) presented a quasipolynomial-time learner for $\mathsf{AC}^0$ under the uniform distribution. However, obtaining comparable learning guarantees for broader classes of correlated distributions has remained a longstanding challenge. Recently, Chandrasekaran, Gaitonde, Moitra, and Vasilyan (arXiv 2026) extended these guarantees to Gibbs distributions on bounded-degree graphical models with both strong spatial mixing and polynomial growth.
  In this paper, we give a quasipolynomial-time learner for $\mathsf{AC}^0$ under graphical models that admit efficient local samplers, circumventing the polynomial-growth requirement in prior work. The key ingredient is a new low-degree approximation for Gibbs distributions, established by simulating and suitably truncating the classical Glauber dynamics. As applications, this framework yields learners for two-spin systems, including the hard-core model and Ising model, on arbitrary bounded-degree graphs, in regimes approaching their respective sampling thresholds.

中文摘要翻译: 学习常数深度电路的问题对计算学习理论具有深远意义。在一项开创性工作中，Linial、Mansour和Nisan（J. ACM 1993）通过引入低度算法，提出了在均匀分布下拟多项式时间学习AC⁰电路的方法。然而，将类似的学习保证推广到更广泛的相关分布类别一直是一个长期挑战。最近，Chandrasekaran、Gaitonde、Moitra和Vasilyan（arXiv 2026）将这些保证扩展到有界度图模型上同时满足强空间混合和多项式增长的吉布斯分布。在本文中，我们提出了一个拟多项式时间学习器，用于在具有高效局部采样器的图模型下学习AC⁰电路，从而绕过了先前工作中对多项式增长的要求。关键要素是通过模拟并适当截断经典Glauber动力学，建立吉布斯分布的一种新的低度近似。作为应用，该框架在任意有界度图上，针对二自旋系统（包括硬核模型和伊辛模型），在接近各自采样阈值的参数区域上都能有效地进行学习。

</details>


### [135] [Write-Protected Discrete Bottlenecks for Language-Grounded World Models: A Structural Limitation and Sufficient Fix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08312)
*Jiayi Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示语言梯度注入Gumbel-Softmax离散符号瓶颈会导致结构性崩溃（符号坍缩至2.2/64个），并提出三层次约束架构（梯度切断+无梯度语义表+DP-Means聚类）实现97.2%的语义绑定准确率，仅需<2M参数且无需LLM微调。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前主流范式（RT-2、Octo、PaLM-E等）假设语言梯度可以直接塑造世界模型的符号表征，但这一假设的安全性未经检验。本文旨在验证该假设是否可靠，若不可靠，则刻画防止失败的最小架构约束。

Method: 提出三层约束架构：(1) 梯度切断（z.detach()），阻止语言信号反向传播到符号瓶颈；(2) 无梯度语义通道——非参数化记忆表（Dict[symbol -> Counter[label]]），用共现计数替代梯度绑定；(3) DP-Means流式聚类处理符号碰撞，自动分裂子簇。

Result: (1) 标准GumbelBottleneck在4/5种子中符号坍缩至2.2/64个，五种抗坍缩策略准确率≤9.2%；(2) 三层架构在32个种子中零符号坍缩，语义绑定准确率79-100%；(3) 完整三层达97.2%接地准确率，无第三层仅22.2%；(4) 在三种编码器架构（CNN、V-JEPA 300M、CLIP ViT-L）、两种环境、三种纹理条件下均有效。

Conclusion: 在GumbelBottleneck家族中，语言梯度与离散符号瓶颈的结合存在结构性失败而非优化问题；所提出的三层次约束架构是解决这一问题的充分条件，实现了高效、鲁棒的语言-世界模型符号对接。

Original Abstract: How should language interface with a world model's discrete symbol system? The dominant paradigm -- end-to-end injection of LLM/VLM features into robot world models (RT-2, Octo, PaLM-E) -- implicitly assumes that language gradients can directly shape physical symbol representations. We ask whether this assumption is safe, find that it is not, and characterize the minimal architectural constraint that prevents the failure. Any language gradient entering a Gumbel-softmax-based discrete symbol bottleneck forces a structural trade-off: the vanilla estimator collapses to 2.2/64 symbols (4/5 seeds), while five anti-collapse strategies maintain diversity but fail to learn semantic labels (all <= 9.2% accuracy). No tested GumbelBottleneck variant achieves both objectives simultaneously. Within this family of discrete bottlenecks, the failure is structural rather than a matter of optimization. We characterize a sufficient set of three constraints that prevent the failure: (1) cut the gradient chain (z.detach()), preventing language signals from reaching the symbol bottleneck; (2) provide a gradient-free semantic channel -- a non-parametric Memory Table (Dict[symbol -> Counter[label]], zero parameters, zero gradients) where co-occurrence counting replaces gradient-based binding; (3) handle symbol collisions via DP-Means streaming clustering for automatic sub-cluster splitting. All three layers together achieve 97.2% grounding accuracy vs. 22.2% without Layer 3. Across two experiments spanning 74 independent runs, we demonstrate zero symbol collapse in all 32 seeds, with the blackboard achieving 79-100% semantic binding across three encoder architectures (CNN, V-JEPA 300M, CLIP ViT-L), two environments, and three texture conditions. The fix trains fewer than 2M parameters and requires no LLM fine-tuning.

中文摘要翻译: 语言应如何与世界模型的离散符号系统对接？主流范式——将LLM/VLM特征端到端注入机器人世界模型（RT-2、Octo、PaLM-E）——隐含地假设语言梯度可以直接塑造物理符号表征。我们提出疑问：这一假设是否安全？我们发现并不安全，并刻画了防止该失败的最小架构约束。任何进入基于Gumbel-Softmax的离散符号瓶颈的语言梯度都会迫使一种结构性权衡：标准估计量在4/5的种子中坍缩至2.2/64个符号，而五种抗坍缩策略虽能保持多样性却无法学习语义标签（准确率均≤9.2%）。所有被测试的GumbelBottleneck变体均无法同时实现两个目标。在这类离散瓶颈家族中，失败是结构性的而非优化问题。我们刻画了防止失败的一组充分的三约束条件：(1) 切断梯度链（z.detach()），阻止语言信号到达符号瓶颈；(2) 提供无梯度语义通道——一个非参数化记忆表（Dict[symbol -> Counter[label]]，零参数、零梯度），其中共现计数替代了基于梯度的绑定；(3) 通过DP-Means流式聚类处理符号碰撞，实现自动子簇分裂。三层共同实现了97.2%的接地准确率，而无第三层时仅为22.2%。在涵盖74次独立运行的两个实验中，我们在全部32个种子中展示了零符号坍缩，黑板架构在三种编码器架构（CNN、V-JEPA 300M、CLIP ViT-L）、两种环境和三种纹理条件下实现了79-100%的语义绑定。该修复训练参数少于2M且无需LLM微调。

</details>


### [136] [ArtMine: Discovering and Formalizing Artistic Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08331)
*Kaustubh Kumar,Ashutosh Ranjan,Vivek Srivastava,Blessin Varkey,Shirish Karande*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ArtMine框架，从碎片化的历史证据（档案、研究草图、信件等）中推断并形式化艺术创作过程，通过Peircean溯因推理智能体生成证据支持的生产步骤，转化为构图图与渲染提示，并通过自反思优化，最终实现对艺术工作流的可解释、可审计的建模。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有生成式AI仅能建模最终艺术品，而无法捕捉创作背后的迭代决策、材料操作与上下文影响；且艺术工作流仅被碎片化地记录在多种异构历史资料中，难以被计算化形式化表达。

Method: 将异构历史证据整合为结构化知识库；基于Peircean溯因推理智能体从证据中推断生产步骤；将步骤转换为构图图与渲染提示；通过自反思机制比较生成结果与参考艺术品之间的差异进行优化。

Result: 在跨艺术家、跨艺术流派的多来源开放历史资料上进行初步概念验证案例研究，证明碎片化的文献证据能够支撑连贯、可解释、可审计的艺术工作流表示。

Conclusion: 通过建模创作过程而非仅最终成品，本文推动了以过程为中心的人机协同创意系统的发展，可支持艺术解读、创意教育、反思性协作及文化生产的计算研究。

Original Abstract: Understanding how artworks are created requires reasoning about the iterative decisions, material operations, and contextual influences that shape artistic production. While recent generative AI systems can synthesize artworks with high fidelity, they primarily model distributions over finished artifacts rather than the creative processes underlying their creation. In practice, artistic workflows are only partially documented through fragmented sources such as archival records, preparatory studies, correspondence, etc., making process-level understanding difficult to formalize computationally. In this work, we introduce ArtMine, a framework for discovering and formalizing artistic processes from heterogeneous historical evidence. Our approach synthesizes heterogeneous artwork evidence into a structured repository, from which a Peircean abductive agent infers evidence-grounded production steps. These steps are converted into a compositional graph and rendering prompt, then optimized through self-reflection over deviations between the generated and reference artworks. We provide a preliminary proof-of-concept case study using open-domain historical sources across multiple artists and artistic movements, demonstrating that fragmented documentary evidence can support coherent, interpretable, and auditable representations of artistic workflows. By modeling creative processes rather than only final artifacts, our work moves toward process-centred human-AI co-creativity systems that can support artistic interpretation, creative education, reflective collaboration, and computational studies of cultural production.

中文摘要翻译: 理解艺术品的创作过程需要推理塑造艺术生产的迭代决策、材料操作和上下文影响。尽管近期的生成式AI系统能够以高保真度合成艺术品，但它们主要建模的是成品艺术品的分布，而非其背后创作过程的建模。在实践中，艺术工作流仅通过档案记录、研究草图、信件等碎片化来源被部分记录，使得过程层面的理解难以被计算化形式化。在本文中，我们提出了ArtMine，一个从异构历史证据中发现并形式化艺术过程的框架。我们的方法将异构的艺术品证据整合为结构化知识库，从中一个Peircean溯因推理智能体推断出基于证据的生产步骤。这些步骤被转换为构图图与渲染提示，随后通过自反思机制在生成结果与参考艺术品之间的偏差上进行优化。我们提供了一个基于跨艺术家、跨艺术流派的开放域历史来源的初步概念验证案例研究，证明了碎片化的文献证据能够支撑连贯、可解释且可审计的艺术工作流表示。通过建模创作过程而不仅仅是最终成品，我们的工作推动了以过程为中心的人机协同创意系统的发展，能够支持艺术解读、创意教育、反思性协作以及文化生产的计算研究。

</details>


### [137] [AutoAnchor: Stable Diffusion Unlearning Using Cross-Attention as a Manifold Surrogate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08337)
*Siyuan Wen,Jiahao Zeng,Ningning Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为Mysys的两阶段框架，通过自动合成流形近端锚点解决扩散模型遗忘中的不稳定问题，显著提升了概念移除效果（CLIP分数提升31.04%）和模型效用（提升4.18%），并能无缝集成到现有遗忘方法中提升其性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有扩散模型遗忘技术存在两类问题：基于锚点的方法依赖人工选择的语义锚点，容易导致有偏遗忘；无锚点的方法由于缺乏对潜空间更新的约束，导致遗忘不稳定。本文从流形假设出发，理论证明缺少流形近端锚点会引发显著的法向空间漂移，从而降低遗忘性能。

Method: 提出Mysys两阶段框架：第一阶段自动合成流形近端锚点；第二阶段利用这些锚点进行稳定遗忘。为解决直接几何流形优化计算不可行的问题，提出一种新颖的交叉注意力一致性损失，作为流形近邻的高效替代代理。

Result: 实验结果表明，Mysys在多个基线方法上实现了鲁棒且无偏的遗忘，目标概念移除的CLIP分数提升高达31.04%，非目标效用提升高达4.18%。集成到现有扩散遗忘方法后，概念移除和效用平均分别提升6.30%和6.65%。

Conclusion: 本文从理论层面形式化了扩散遗忘的不稳定性问题，提出的Mysys框架通过自动合成流形近端锚点有效实现了稳定遗忘，且具有良好的通用性和集成性。

Original Abstract: Diffusion unlearning is essential for mitigating the generation of harmful or copyrighted content in text-to-image models. Current diffusion unlearning techniques determine the model update direction by either using alternatives of the target concept as an anchor or using empty prompts. The anchor-based method relies on manually and semantically-chosen anchors that risk biased unlearning, while the anchor-free method inherently suffers from unrobust unlearning due to unconstrained latent updates. In this work, we theoretically formalize such unstable diffusion unlearning issues under the manifold hypothesis and prove that lacking a manifold-proximal anchor inevitably induces significant normal-space drift that degrades unlearning performance. To achieve stable unlearning, we propose \mysysn, a two-stage framework that automatically synthesizes manifold-proximal anchors. However, direct geometric manifold optimization is computationally intractable. To address this challenge, \mysys introduces a novel cross-attention consistency loss which serves as a highly efficient surrogate of manifold proximity. Experimental results demonstrate that \mysys effectively achieves robust and unbiased unlearning across various state-of-the-art baselines, significantly improving targeted concept removal (by up to 31.04\% in CLIP score) and non-target utility (by up to 4.18\% in CLIP score). Moreover, \mysys can also be easily integrated into existing diffusion unlearning methods to enhance their unlearning performance (by 6.30\% for concept removal and 6.65\% for utility on average).

中文摘要翻译: 扩散遗忘对于减轻文本到图像模型中生成有害或受版权保护的内容至关重要。当前的扩散遗忘技术要么使用目标概念的替代作为锚点，要么使用空提示来确定模型更新方向。基于锚点的方法依赖于人工和语义选择的锚点，存在有偏遗忘的风险；而无锚点的方法由于潜空间更新缺乏约束，本质上存在遗忘不稳健的问题。本文在流形假设下理论形式化了这类不稳定的扩散遗忘问题，并证明缺乏流形近端锚点不可避免地会导致显著的法向空间漂移，从而降低遗忘性能。为了实现稳定遗忘，我们提出Mysys，一个自动合成流形近端锚点的两阶段框架。然而，直接的几何流形优化在计算上不可行。为应对这一挑战，Mysys引入了一种新颖的交叉注意力一致性损失，作为流形近邻的高效替代代理。实验结果表明，Mysys在多种先进基线上有效实现了稳健且无偏的遗忘，显著提升了目标概念移除（CLIP分数提升高达31.04%）和非目标效用（CLIP分数提升高达4.18%）。此外，Mysys还可以轻松集成到现有的扩散遗忘方法中，以增强其遗忘性能（概念移除和效用平均分别提升6.30%和6.65%）。

</details>


### [138] [Spectral Analysis of Dueling Q-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08340)
*Donghwan Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对无正则化、无投影的常步长表格型决斗Q学习（Dueling Q-learning）提供了完整的理论分析，包括确定性版本的精确切换线性系统表示和随机版本的有限时间误差界，揭示了价值和优势更新在Q函数中的作用机制。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 虽然决斗Q学习在深度强化学习中已被广泛应用，但其理论基础薄弱。现有工作仅分析了正则化形式的表格型决斗Q学习，对纯表格更新的理解尚不完整。

Method: 推导了确定性决斗Q学习的精确切换线性系统表示，以及采样随机版本的期望有限时间误差界。

Result: 建立了无正则化、无投影常步长决斗Q学习的收敛保证；阐明了价值函数更新和优势函数更新分别以不同增益作用于Q函数的动作公共分量（价值函数）和动作差分分量（优势函数）。

Conclusion: 该分析增强了对决斗Q学习的理论理解，为中心化表格分解提供了直接解释，并首次给出了纯表格更新的收敛保证。

Original Abstract: Q-learning is a fundamental algorithm in reinforcement learning (RL) for solving discounted Markov decision processes (MDPs) when the transition kernel is unknown. The deep Q-network (DQN) extends Q-learning by using a deep neural network for Q-function approximation, which makes Q-learning applicable to more practical high-dimensional problems. Dueling Q-learning decomposes the Q-function into a value function and an advantage function and learns the two components jointly, which can improve learning efficiency. However, the theoretical understanding of dueling Q-learning is still limited. Recent work has initiated an analysis of tabular dueling Q-learning, but existing guarantees focus on a regularized formulation and leave the pure tabular update less completely understood. This paper strengthens that line of analysis by adding a direct interpretation of the centered tabular decomposition and by establishing convergence guarantees for the unregularized, unprojected constant step-size recursion. In particular, we derive an exact switching linear system representation for deterministic dueling Q-learning and a finite-time error bound in expectation for the sampled stochastic version. The analysis clarifies how the value and advantage updates act as different gains on the action-common (value function) and action-differential (advantage function) components of the Q-function.

中文摘要翻译: Q学习是强化学习中求解折扣马尔可夫决策过程（当转移核未知时）的基础算法。深度Q网络通过使用深度神经网络进行Q函数近似来扩展Q学习，使Q学习适用于更高维的实际问题。决斗Q学习将Q函数分解为价值函数和优势函数，并联合学习这两个分量，从而提高学习效率。然而，对决斗Q学习的理论理解仍十分有限。近期工作启动了对表格型决斗Q学习的分析，但现有保证聚焦于正则化形式，对纯表格更新的理解尚不完整。本文通过添加对中心化表格分解的直接解释，并为无正则化、无投影的常步长递归建立收敛保证，从而加强了该方向的分析。具体而言，我们推导了确定性决斗Q学习的精确切换线性系统表示，以及采样随机版本在期望意义下的有限时间误差界。该分析阐明了价值函数更新和优势函数更新如何分别以不同增益作用于Q函数的动作公共分量（价值函数）和动作差分分量（优势函数）。

</details>


### [139] [Certified Interventional Fidelity: Anytime-Valid, Adaptive Evaluation of Causal Claims in Mechanistic Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08349)
*Amir Asiaee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出认证干预保真度（CIF），一个为可解释性评估提供统计保障的框架，通过将报告量化为因果估计量并给出置信区间和随时有效的置信序列，包括在自适应干预采样下的保障。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前机械可解释性评估通常只报告点估计，忽略了有限采样和评估选择带来的不确定性，难以区分报告的因果声明是否稳定。

Method: CIF首先将报告量写为因果估计量（在有界评分上对指定输入分布和干预分布的期望），然后提供置信区间和随时有效的置信序列，并通过有界混合重要性加权处理自适应干预采样。实例化包括Hoeffding型序列和方差自适应投注序列。

Result: 方差自适应投注序列将认证成本降低10-30倍；在MNIST抽象和GPT-2 Small IOI电路上，CIF能够认证高保真度声明、揭示方法间差异在统计上不显著，并使对干预分布的敏感性显式化。

Conclusion: CIF为可解释性评估提供了一种严格的统计框架，使评估结果具有统计保障，能够区分真正的因果效应与采样噪声。

Original Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability often evaluates explanations by intervening on a model: swapping hidden states, patching activations, ablating components, or comparing a compressed model to the original one. These experiments are usually summarized by a point estimate, even though the evaluation may be monitored while it runs or adapted toward suspected failures. This makes it hard to tell whether a reported fidelity or patching effect is a stable causal claim or a consequence of finite sampling and evaluation choices. We introduce Certified Interventional Fidelity (CIF), a statistical layer for interventional interpretability evaluations. CIF first writes the quantity being reported as a causal estimand: an expectation of a bounded score over a stated input distribution and a stated intervention distribution. It then provides confidence intervals and anytime-valid confidence sequences for this estimand, including under adaptive intervention sampling via bounded mixture importance weighting. We instantiate CIF with Hoeffding-style sequences and variance-adaptive betting sequences, the latter reducing certification cost by 10-30x in our experiments. On MNIST abstractions and GPT-2 Small IOI circuits, CIF certifies high-fidelity claims, shows when apparent method differences are not statistically supported, and makes sensitivity to the intervention distribution explicit.

中文摘要翻译: 机械可解释性通常通过干预模型来评估解释：交换隐藏状态、修补激活、消融组件，或将压缩模型与原始模型进行比较。这些实验通常用一个点估计来总结，尽管评估可能在运行过程中被监控或针对可疑失败进行调整。这使得难以判断报告的保真度或修补效果是稳定的因果声明，还是有限采样和评估选择的后果。我们引入了认证干预保真度（CIF），一个用于干预式可解释性评估的统计层。CIF首先将报告的量写为因果估计量：在一个指定的输入分布和一个指定的干预分布上，对有界评分的期望。然后，它为这个估计量提供置信区间和随时有效的置信序列，包括通过有界混合重要性加权在自适应干预采样下的保障。我们使用Hoeffding型序列和方差自适应投注序列实例化CIF，后者在我们的实验中降低了10-30倍的认证成本。在MNIST抽象和GPT-2 Small IOI电路上，CIF认证了高保真度声明，揭示了看似方法差异在统计上并不显著，并使对干预分布的敏感性变得显式化。

</details>


### [140] [Self-Adaptive Anomaly Detection with Reinforcement Learning and Human Feedback in Connected Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08373)
*Matthias Weiß,Athreya Hosahalli Prakash,Maurice Artelt,Falk Dettinger,Nasser Jazdi,Michael Weyrich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向自主信息物理系统的在线异常检测框架，集成深度Q网络选择检测器、统计漂移检测器和人机协同重训练机制，在联网车辆自动泊车应用中取得优于单一检测器的性能，并能适应软件更新带来的概念漂移而不遗忘历史知识。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 联网车辆等自主信息物理系统在运行中会经历空中更新、配置变化和工作负载转移，导致正常行为定义持续演变，静态诊断方法性能会无声退化。现有方法要么单独处理模型自适应，要么单独处理操作员集成，缺乏统一的协调监督回路。

Method: 提出三个协调机制：(1) 带自注意力的因子化深度Q网络，从候选池中为每个微服务选择最合适的检测器，利用微服务拓扑中的服务间依赖关系；(2) 三个统计漂移检测器组成的集成，三者一致才发出警报，优先保证精确率而非召回率；(3) 人机协同重训练机制，基于待定过渡缓冲区和60/40优先回放策略，允许操作员融入专家知识同时保留系统对先前数据分布的学习响应。

Result: 在连接车辆测试台上评估自动泊车应用的七个后端微服务：注意力增强的智能体F1分数达0.69，而任何单一检测器均匀应用时最高仅0.11。软件更新引致概念漂移后F1降至0.52；操作员触发重训练后，在新分布上性能恢复至0.65，同时在先前分布上保持0.69，展示了持续适应能力且不产生灾难性遗忘。

Conclusion: 所提框架通过在线、协调的检测器选择、漂移检测和人机协同重训练，能有效应对自主信息物理系统的行为演化，在概念漂移发生后通过操作员介入恢复性能，同时保持对历史知识的记忆。

Original Abstract: Connected vehicles are autonomous cyber-physical systems whose behavior must be continuously monitored during operation to detect deviations from normal operation before they propagate into failures. Such evaluation is challenging because the systems themselves evolve: over-the-air updates, configuration changes, and shifting workloads alter the definition of normal behavior, causing static diagnostic methods to degrade silently over time. Existing approaches typically address either automated model adaptation or operator integration in isolation, rather than as a single coordinated supervisory loop.
  This paper presents an online anomaly detection framework for autonomous CPS that integrates three coordinated mechanisms. A factorized deep Q-network with self-attention selects the most suitable detector from a candidate pool for each monitored service, exploiting inter-service dependencies in the microservice topology. An ensemble of three statistical drift detectors monitors the input distribution and raises an alarm only when all three concur, prioritizing precision over recall. A human-in-the-loop retraining mechanism, built around a pending transition buffer and a 60/40 prioritized replay strategy, allows the operator to incorporate expert knowledge while preserving the system's learned response to prior data distributions.
  The framework is evaluated on a connected-vehicle testbed running an automated valet parking application across seven backend microservices. The attention-augmented agent achieves an F1 score of 0.69, compared to at most 0.11 for any single detector applied uniformly. Following a real software update that induces measurable concept drift, F1 drops to 0.52; after operator-triggered retraining, performance recovers to 0.65 on the new distribution while remaining at 0.69 on the prior one, demonstrating sustained adaptation without catastrophic forgetting.

中文摘要翻译: 联网车辆是自主信息物理系统，其行为必须在运行期间持续监控，以便在偏差传播为故障之前检测出偏离正常操作的情况。这一评估具有挑战性，因为系统本身在演化：空中更新、配置变化和工作负载转移改变了正常行为的定义，导致静态诊断方法随时间无声退化。现有方法通常要么处理自动模型自适应，要么处理操作员集成，而非作为单个协调的监督回路。
本文提出一个面向自主信息物理系统的在线异常检测框架，集成了三种协调机制。一种带有自注意力的因子化深度Q网络从候选池中为每个受监控的服务选择最合适的检测器，利用微服务拓扑中的服务间依赖关系。三个统计漂移检测器组成的集成监控输入分布，仅当三者一致时才发出警报，优先保证精确率而非召回率。一种基于待定过渡缓冲区和60/40优先回放策略的人机协同重训练机制，允许操作员融入专家知识，同时保留系统对先前数据分布的学习响应。
该框架在运行自动泊车应用的联网车辆测试台上进行评估，涉及七个后端微服务。注意力增强的智能体F1分数达到0.69，而任何单一检测器均匀应用时最高仅为0.11。在一次引起可测概念漂移的真实软件更新后，F1降至0.52；操作员触发重训练后，在新分布上性能恢复至0.65，同时在先前分布上保持0.69，展示了持续的适应能力且不会灾难性遗忘。

</details>


### [141] [Eigenvalue Calibration for Semantic Embeddings of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08377)
*Sebastian G. Gruber,Nassim Walha,Francis Bach,Florian Buettner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种校准语义嵌入特征值的新框架，通过将LLM与语义嵌入解释为密度矩阵预测器，并应用温度缩放校准特征值，解决了传统分类概率校准方法无法直接迁移到特征值的问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统分类概率的校准结果无法直接迁移到语义嵌入特征值上，而特征值已成为最先进的不确定性量化方法中的关键工具，因此需要专门的校准框架。

Method: 将LLM与生成答案的语义嵌入共同解释为密度矩阵预测器，通过对特征值应用温度缩放（temperature scaling）来校准密度矩阵预测器；建立了熵-风险等价性，推导了特征值专用中心校准不等式，证明温度缩放特征值在最小化恰当评分风险时优化校准。

Result: 在多种真实场景下的实验表明，当前LLM普遍存在过度自信（系统性地过于乐观），同时实验结果验证了理论发现。

Conclusion: 该工作推进了语义嵌入不确定性量化的理论基础和实践方法。

Original Abstract: Uncertainty quantification is central to the reliable deployment of large language models (LLMs), and eigenvalues of semantic embeddings have recently emerged as a key tool in state-of-the-art methods. However, conventional calibration results developed for classification probabilities cannot be directly transferred to eigenvalues. We address this gap by proposing a novel framework for calibrating the eigenvalues of semantic embeddings. We interpret LLMs combined with semantic embeddings of their generated answers as density matrix predictors, and we propose a novel approach to calibrate density matrix predictors by applying temperature scaling to their eigenvalues. We establish entropy-risk equivalence under calibration, derive a central calibration inequality specific to eigenvalues, and prove that temperature-scaled eigenvalues optimize calibration when minimizing proper score risks. Experiments on a variety of real-world settings show that current LLMs are systematically overconfident, and validate our theoretical findings. Together, these results advance the foundations and practice of uncertainty quantification for semantic embeddings.

中文摘要翻译: 不确定性量化对于大型语言模型（LLM）的可靠部署至关重要，而语义嵌入的特征值最近已成为最先进方法中的关键工具。然而，为分类概率开发的传统校准结果无法直接迁移到特征值上。我们通过提出一种新颖的语义嵌入特征值校准框架来填补这一空白。我们将LLM与其生成答案的语义嵌入共同解释为密度矩阵预测器，并提出一种通过对特征值应用温度缩放来校准密度矩阵预测器的新方法。我们建立了校准下的熵-风险等价性，推导了专门针对特征值的中心校准不等式，并证明了温度缩放特征值在最小化恰当评分风险时能够优化校准。在多种真实场景下的实验表明，当前LLM系统性地存在过度自信，并验证了我们的理论发现。这些结果共同推进了语义嵌入不确定性量化的基础理论和实践方法。

</details>


### [142] [Dynamics of Gradient Descent with Large Step Size Near a Manifold of Flat Minima](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08380)
*Lachlan Ewen MacDonald,René Vidal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将梯度下降大学习率理论从标量输出过参数化最小二乘和孤立平坦最小值扩展到向量输出和多观察值回归，以及平坦最小值流形邻域，并应用于深度矩阵分解。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 经典梯度下降分析要求步长小于尖锐度倒数的两倍，但深度神经网络训练中常违反此条件。现有工作仅在单标量输出的过参数化最小二乘和孤立平坦最小值邻域建立了理论，需推广到更一般的向量输出场景和平坦最小值流形场景。

Method: (1) 将正规形和三个收敛定理从标量输出推广到向量输出的过参数化最小二乘；(2) 从孤立平坦最小值邻域推广到平坦最小值流形邻域；(3) 通过一种新颖的奇异偏微分方程求解方法克服技术挑战；(4) 将框架应用于深度矩阵分解。

Result: (1) 在向量输出和平坦最小值流形邻域下推广了正规形和三个收敛定理；(2) 证明了深度矩阵分解中平坦最小值集合构成一个球面乘积上的纤维丛；(3) 证明了尖锐度沿该流形是Morse-Bott的。

Conclusion: 本文扩展了大步长梯度下降理论的应用范围，使其能处理更实际的向量输出场景和平坦最小值流形场景，并为深度矩阵分解提供了新的结构见解。

Original Abstract: An important quantity in the theory of gradient descent (GD) is the \emph{sharpness}, defined as the largest eigenvalue of the objective Hessian. Classical analyses typically require the step size to be uniformly smaller than twice the reciprocal of the sharpness, but this condition is frequently violated in the training of deep neural networks. Recent work bridges this gap in the setting of overparametrised least-squares with a \emph{single scalar output}, providing a normal form for large-step GD in a neighbourhood of an \emph{isolated} flat minimum and establishing three corresponding convergence results. In this paper, we extend this theory in two directions: (1) to overparametrised least-squares with \emph{vector-valued outputs} (including regression with arbitrarily many observations), and (2) to a neighbourhood of a \emph{manifold} of flat minima (which we show is essential for applications such as matrix factorisation). We generalise both the normal form and all three convergence theorems of \cite{macdonaldeos} to this broader setting, overcoming several technical challenges, including the solution of a singular partial differential equation via a novel method that may be of independent interest. We further show that our framework applies to deep matrix factorisation under mild assumptions, yielding several new structural results. In particular, we prove that the set of flat minima forms a fibre bundle over a product of spheres, and that the sharpness is Morse-Bott along this manifold.

中文摘要翻译: 梯度下降理论中的一个重要量是\emph{尖锐度}，定义为目标黑塞矩阵的最大特征值。经典分析通常要求步长均匀小于尖锐度倒数的两倍，但此条件在深度神经网络训练中经常被违反。最近的工作在\emph{单标量输出}的过参数化最小二乘设定下弥合了这一差距，在\emph{孤立}平坦最小值邻域内为大步长梯度下降提供了正规形，并建立了相应的三个收敛结果。本文在两个方向上扩展了这一理论：(1) 扩展到\emph{向量值输出}的过参数化最小二乘（包括具有任意多观测值的回归），以及(2) 扩展到\emph{流形}上的平坦最小值邻域（我们证明这对于矩阵分解等应用至关重要）。我们将\cite{macdonaldeos}的正规形和所有三个收敛定理推广到这一更广泛的设定，克服了若干技术挑战，包括通过一种新颖方法求解奇异偏微分方程——该方法可能具有独立的研究价值。我们进一步证明，在温和假设下，我们的框架适用于深度矩阵分解，产生了若干新的结构结果。特别地，我们证明了平坦最小值集合构成一个球面乘积上的纤维丛，并且尖锐度沿该流形是Morse-Bott的。

</details>


### [143] [Beyond Backpropagation: Monte Carlo Method Can Train Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08406)
*Hong Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明，最简单的蒙特卡洛算法——在单GPU上随机变异一个参数，若损失下降则保留，否则重试——可以实际训练深度网络。该方法无需梯度、批归一化或残差连接，可直接训练超过20层的深度网络，支持纯剪枝训练、离散权重、高斯激活函数等场景，并在图像分类和语言建模任务上验证了可行性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 反向传播依赖梯度带来了固有的梯度消失/爆炸问题，寻找无梯度方法一直是AI领域的目标。本文旨在探索一种最简单的无梯度方法能否有效训练深度网络。

Method: 最简单的蒙特卡洛算法：在单GPU上随机变异网络中的一个参数，如果损失下降则保留该变异，否则回退并重试。该方法无需梯度计算、批归一化或残差连接。

Result: 在超过20层的深度网络、单隐层宽网络（多达16384个隐藏神经元）以及简单的Transformer架构上验证了可行性；支持纯剪枝训练、离散权重、高斯激活函数等非常规场景；揭示了深度网络存在大量冗余。

Conclusion: 这种简单的无梯度方法为理解神经网络的自组织和学习机制提供了补充视角，也为构建受物理启发的深度学习系统提供了替代路径。

Original Abstract: Backpropagation (BP) dominates deep learning training, but its reliance on gradients brings inherent troubles -- vanishing and exploding gradients. The pursuit of gradient-free methods has long been a goal in the field of artificial intelligence. This paper shows that indeed the simplest Monte Carlo algorithm implemented on a single GPU -- randomly mutate a parameter, keep it if the loss decreases, otherwise retry -- can practically train deep networks. This gradient-free method does not even need common techniques such as batch normalization or residual connections to directly train sufficiently deep networks. More remarkably, its flexibility extends to several nontrivial scenarios: it enables pure pruning training, supports discrete weights, accommodates unconventional transfer functions such as Gaussian, and reveals the substantial redundancy of deep networks. We have demonstrated its feasibility on deep networks with more than 20 layers, single-hidden-layer wide networks with up to 16,384 hidden neurons, and even a simple Transformer architecture trained on both image classification (MNIST) and character-level language modeling (Tiny Shakespeare). This simple gradient-free method may offer a complementary perspective for understanding the self-organization and learning mechanisms of neural networks, and also provides an alternative route for building physically inspired deep learning systems.

中文摘要翻译: 反向传播（BP）主导着深度学习训练，但其对梯度的依赖带来了固有的问题——梯度消失与梯度爆炸。追求无梯度方法一直是人工智能领域的目标。本文证明，在单个GPU上实现的最简单的蒙特卡洛算法——随机变异一个参数，如果损失下降则保留该变异，否则重试——实际上可以训练深度网络。这种无梯度方法甚至不需要批归一化或残差连接等常用技术，就能直接训练足够深的网络。更为显著的是，其灵活性扩展到了若干非平凡场景：它支持纯剪枝训练、兼容离散权重、适应高斯等非常规传递函数，并揭示了深度网络的实质冗余。我们已在超过20层的深度网络、单隐层宽网络（隐藏神经元多达16384个）以及简单的Transformer架构上证明了其可行性，这些模型分别在图像分类（MNIST）和字符级语言建模（Tiny Shakespeare）任务上进行了训练。这种简单的无梯度方法可能为理解神经网络的自组织和学习机制提供一种补充视角，也为构建受物理启发的深度学习系统提供了一条替代路径。

</details>


### [144] [Predicting Male Fertility Using Machine Learning: A Semen Parameters Based Analysis with the VISEM Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08429)
*Shahnawaz Qureshi,Raja Khurram Shahzad,Muhammad Fozan,Emal Kawal,Syed Aziz Shah,Sattam Al-Anazi,Syed MuhammadZeeshan Iqbal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究利用机器学习算法（基于LazyPredict框架）对男性生育力状态进行分类，在VISEM数据集上，Nearest Centroid分类器达到94.2%的准确率，优于SVM和QDA等模型，表明机器学习可辅助临床精液质量评估。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 男性不育症在生殖健康中常被低估，而精液分析是临床评估的核心。本研究旨在利用机器学习算法基于关键精液参数（精子浓度、活力、形态）对生育力状态进行自动分类，以提供快速、客观的评估手段，辅助临床决策。

Method: 使用VISEM数据集（85名参与者的精液样本，按WHO标准分为Fertile、Sub-Fertile、Infertile三类），经过预处理和特征工程后，利用LazyPredict框架训练和评估40多种分类模型，采用5折交叉验证和多类ROC-AUC分析验证模型鲁棒性。

Result: 在40多种算法中，Nearest Centroid分类器表现最优，准确率达到94.2%，优于支持向量机（SVM）和二次判别分析（QDA）等模型。

Conclusion: 机器学习模型能够提供快速、准确、客观的精液质量评估，有望支持男科和辅助生殖技术中的临床决策，增强生育力诊断并为患者个体化治疗策略提供信息。

Original Abstract: Male infertility is a significant yet often underdiagnosed aspect of reproductive health, with semen analysis serving as the cornerstone of clinical evaluation. To address this problem, this study investigates the use of machine learning algorithms to classify male fertility status based on key semen parameters, i.e., sperm concentration, motility, and morphology, using the VISEM dataset. This dataset includes semen samples from 85 participants, classified into three categories, i.e., Fertile, Sub-Fertile, and Infertile, according to the World Health Organization's criteria. After pre-processing and feature engineering, the dataset was used to train and assess multiple classification models using the LazyPredict framework. Among the more than 40 algorithms tested, the Nearest Centroid classifier achieved an accuracy of 94.2%, outperforming other models such as Support Vector Machines and Quadratic Discriminant Analysis. The model's robustness was validated using 5-fold cross-validation and multiclass ROC-AUC analysis. This study illustrates that machine learning models can provide fast, accurate, and objective assessments of semen quality, potentially supporting clinical decision-making in andrology and assisted reproductive technologies. These findings emphasize the growing potential of machine learning to enhance fertility diagnostics and inform patient-specific treatment strategies.

中文摘要翻译: 男性不育症是生殖健康中一个显著但常被低估的问题，精液分析是临床评估的基石。针对这一问题，本研究探索了使用机器学习算法基于关键精液参数（即精子浓度、活力、形态）对男性生育力状态进行分类，使用VISEM数据集。该数据集包含85名参与者的精液样本，根据世界卫生组织的标准分为三类：可育、亚不育和不育。经过预处理和特征工程后，利用LazyPredict框架训练和评估多个分类模型。在测试的40多种算法中，Nearest Centroid分类器达到了94.2%的准确率，优于支持向量机和二次判别分析等其他模型。通过5折交叉验证和多类ROC-AUC分析验证了模型的鲁棒性。本研究表明，机器学习模型可以提供快速、准确、客观的精液质量评估，可能支持男科和辅助生殖技术中的临床决策。这些发现强调了机器学习在增强生育力诊断和为患者个体化治疗策略提供信息方面日益增长的潜力。

</details>


### [145] [MatBind: A Shared Embedding Space for Multimodal Materials Characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08470)
*Le Yang,Anoop K. Chandran,Jona Östreicher,Evgenii Sovetkin,Adrian Mirza,Sebastien Bompas,Bashir Kazimi,Pascal Friederich,Stefan Kesselheim,Kevin Maik Jablonka,Stefan Sandfeld*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MatBind提出了一种对比学习框架，将晶体结构、模拟X射线衍射图谱、态密度和文本四种材料模态对齐到统一嵌入空间，以晶体结构为物理锚点，实现了零样本跨模态检索，并证明多模态融合能系统性提升检索性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的材料数据模态（原子结构、衍射图谱、电子态密度、自然语言）各自捕获同一物理对象的不同侧面，但实际中被孤立存储和分析，难以跨表征边界进行关联或查询，缺乏统一的对齐框架。

Method: 提出MatBind对比学习框架，以晶体结构作为中心物理锚点，将四种材料模态（晶体结构、基于结构模拟的粉末X射线衍射图、态密度、文本）对齐到统一的嵌入空间中，通过对比学习诱导模态间对齐，即使训练时从未显式配对的模态也能实现零样本跨模态检索。

Result: 学习到的嵌入空间无需显式监督即可根据物理有意义的属性组织材料；查询时组合多种模态能系统性提升检索性能；验证了将异构材料数据视为同一物理实在的互补投影而非孤立数据源是符合底层物理规律的。

Conclusion: 将异构材料数据作为同一物理实体的互补投影进行处理，不仅是实用的选择，而且与底层物理学相一致。

Original Abstract: Fully characterizing a crystalline material requires integrating heterogeneous data sources -- atomic structures, diffraction patterns, electronic density of states, and natural language -- each of which captures a different facet of the same physical object. In practice, however, these modalities are stored and analyzed in isolation, making it difficult to relate or query materials across representational boundaries. We present MatBind, a contrastive learning framework that aligns four materials modalities -- crystal structure, powder X-ray diffraction (pXRD) simulated from structures, density of states (DOS), and text -- into a unified embedding space using crystal structure as the central physical anchor. The framework induces alignment between modalities never explicitly paired during training, enabling emergent zero-shot cross-modal retrieval as a direct consequence of the shared representation. The learned embedding space organizes materials according to physically meaningful properties without explicit supervision, and retrieval performance improves systematically when modalities are combined at query time. These results demonstrate that treating heterogeneous materials data as complementary projections of a single physical reality, rather than as isolated data sources, is not a practical choice but is consistent with the underlying physics.

中文摘要翻译: 全面表征一种晶体材料需要整合异构数据源——原子结构、衍射图谱、电子态密度以及自然语言文本——每种数据源捕获同一物理对象的不同侧面。然而在实践中，这些模态被孤立地存储和分析，使得跨表征边界关联或查询材料变得困难。我们提出了MatBind，一个对比学习框架，它将四种材料模态——晶体结构、基于结构模拟的粉末X射线衍射图谱、态密度和文本——以晶体结构作为中心物理锚点，对齐到一个统一的嵌入空间中。该框架诱导了在训练过程中从未显式配对的模态之间的对齐，从而实现了零样本跨模态检索，这是共享表征的直接结果。学习到的嵌入空间无需显式监督即可根据物理有意义的属性组织材料，并且在查询时组合多种模态能系统性提升检索性能。这些结果表明，将异构材料数据视为单一物理实在的互补投影，而非孤立的数据源，不仅是一种实际选择，而且与底层物理规律相一致。

</details>


### [146] [Frequency-Domain Multi-Modality Transportation Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08475)
*Jiewen Deng,Hangchen Liu,Junchen Li,Boyuan Zhang,Renhe Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出频域多模态交通预测模型FreMo，通过模态级频谱滤波和频域引导的跨模态融合，实现轻量高效的多模态交通预测。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法主要在时域建模或采用粗粒度特征融合，难以处理不同交通模态间频谱特性差异大、跨频交互不均匀的问题，导致多模态交通预测精度受限。

Method: FreMo包含两个核心模块：(1) 模态级频率滤波器(MFF)，自适应地细化各模态的频谱分量，增强有效频段、抑制噪声；(2) 频域引导的协同融合器(FSI)，根据各模态在不同频率上的相对贡献选择性聚合信息，实现有效的跨模态知识共享。整体可即插即用，兼容通用时间序列骨干网络。

Result: 在真实多模态交通数据集上，FreMo一致性地优于现有最先进基线方法，展现出优异的预测性能和良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 显式利用频域进行自适应、选择性的跨模态协同建模，是提升多模态交通预测性能的有效且轻量的方案。

Original Abstract: Multi-modality transportation refers to urban systems composed of multiple transportation modes, such as traffic flow and public transit, whose dynamics are coupled by shared temporal patterns. Accurate multi-modality transportation forecasting remains challenging because (1) different modalities exhibit distinct spectral characteristics and (2) interact unevenly across frequencies, whereas most existing methods operate primarily in the time domain or rely on coarse feature fusion. To address these limitations, we propose a lightweight yet effective Frequency-Domain Multi-Modality modeling (FreMo) that explicitly exploits the frequency domain to enable adaptive and selective cross-modality synergy. FreMo disentangles modality-wise spectral refinement from cross-modality synergy and supports plug-and-play integration with general time series backbones. Specifically, FreMo introduces a Modality-Wise Frequency Filter (MFF) to adaptively refine spectral components within each modality, emphasizing informative frequencies while suppressing noise. FreMo further incorporates a Frequency-Guided Synergy Integrator (FSI) that selectively aggregates information across modalities based on their relative contribution at each frequency, facilitating effective cross-modality knowledge sharing while mitigating negative transfer. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets show that FreMo consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, with superior performance and generalization across diverse forecasting scenarios. The code is available at https://github.com/beginner-sketch/FreMo.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态交通是指由多种交通方式（如交通流和公共交通）组成的城市系统，其动力学通过共享的时间模式相互耦合。准确的多模态交通预测仍然具有挑战性，因为：(1) 不同模态展现出不同的频谱特性；(2) 不同模态在不同频率上交互不均匀，而现有方法大多在时域中操作或依赖粗粒度的特征融合。为解决这些局限，我们提出了一种轻量且有效的频域多模态建模方法（FreMo），该方法显式利用频域实现自适应、选择性的跨模态协同。FreMo将模态级的频谱细化与跨模态协同解耦，并支持与通用时间序列骨干网络的即插即用集成。具体而言，FreMo引入模态级频率滤波器（MFF），自适应地细化每个模态内的频谱分量，增强信息频率的同时抑制噪声。FreMo还进一步引入了频域引导的协同融合器（FSI），该模块根据各模态在每个频率上的相对贡献选择性聚合信息，从而促进有效的跨模态知识共享并减轻负迁移。在真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，FreMo在多种预测场景下一致性地优于现有最先进基线方法，具有优越的性能和泛化能力。代码已开源：https://github.com/beginner-sketch/FreMo。

</details>


### [147] [Ensemble Diversity Optimization for Subjective Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08493)
*Xia Cui,Ziyi Huang,N. R. Abeynayake*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种名为集成多样性优化（EDO）的预测空间框架，通过联合优化集成权重、有效基数和校准，并使用带符号的多样性正则化器，在主观NLP任务中实现对标注者分歧的建模，实验表明该方法在概率校准上显著优于多个基线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 主观性NLP任务中标注者之间存在系统性分歧，现有模型往往忽略或压缩这种不确定性，缺乏能够有效建模和表示分歧的方法。

Method: EDO框架：通过Gumbel-Softmax松弛实现端到端学习集成组成和规模；引入带符号的多样性正则化器（在验证集上调参）以引导优化方向；整合软F1替代损失、类别加权交叉熵处理类别不平衡、以及基于可靠性的多样性度量以调控集成内部变异。

Result: 在四个主观文本分类基准（ArMIS, ConvAbuse, HS-Brexit, MD-Agreement）上，相比Soft-CE、Soft-MD、Top-5 Voting和WEL等基线，EDO大幅降低了交叉熵（40-78%）和Brier分数，同时保持了有竞争力的F1分数，并与标注者分布更一致。

Conclusion: 联合优化集成结构与带符号的多样性正则化器，为监督学习中建模人类主观性提供了一种高效且模型无关的方法。

Original Abstract: Subjective NLP tasks often exhibit systematic annotator disagreement, requiring models that represent uncertainty rather than collapse it. We introduce Ensemble Diversity Optimization (EDO), a prediction-space framework that jointly optimizes ensemble weights, effective cardinality, and calibration through a unified differentiable objective. EDO learns ensemble composition and size end-to-end via Gumbel-Softmax relaxation and incorporates a signed diversity regularizer, tuned on validation data, to steer optimization toward either preserving or suppressing disagreement. This regularization prevents ensemble collapse and enables controlled navigation of the utility-calibration trade-off. The framework integrates a soft F1 surrogate, class-weighted cross-entropy to address imbalance, and reliability-weighted diversity to regulate intra-ensemble variability. Experiments on four subjective text-classification benchmarks (ArMIS, ConvAbuse, HS-Brexit, MD-Agreement) show that EDO substantially improves probabilistic calibration, reducing cross-entropy (40-78% depending on baseline) and lowering Brier scores relative to Soft-CE, Soft-MD, Top-5 Voting, and WEL, while maintaining competitive F1 and better alignment with annotator distributions. These results demonstrate that jointly optimizing ensemble structure with a signed diversity regularizer provides an efficient, model-agnostic approach for modeling human subjectivity in supervised learning.

中文摘要翻译: 主观性自然语言处理任务通常表现出系统性的标注者分歧，这要求模型能够表示不确定性而非将其压缩。我们提出集成多样性优化（EDO），这是一种预测空间框架，通过统一的可微目标联合优化集成权重、有效基数和校准。EDO通过Gumbel-Softmax松弛实现端到端地学习集成组成和规模，并引入一个带符号的多样性正则化器——在验证数据上调参——引导优化方向，以保留或抑制分歧。该正则化防止集成坍缩，并实现对效用-校准权衡的受控导航。该框架整合了软F1替代损失、处理类别不平衡的类别加权交叉熵，以及基于可靠性的多样性度量以调控集成内部变异。在四个主观文本分类基准（ArMIS、ConvAbuse、HS-Brexit、MD-Agreement）上的实验表明，相比Soft-CE、Soft-MD、Top-5 Voting和WEL，EDO显著提升了概率校准质量，降低了交叉熵（根据基线不同降低40-78%）和Brier分数，同时保持了有竞争力的F1分数，并与标注者分布更一致。这些结果表明，联合优化集成结构与带符号的多样性正则化器，为在监督学习中建模人类主观性提供了一种高效且模型无关的方法。

</details>


### [148] [Systematic Evaluation of Learning Rate Scheduling Strategies Across Heterogeneous Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08511)
*Hafsa Mateen,Radu Timofte,Dmitry Ignatov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了学习率调度策略对30种代表性神经网络架构分类准确率的影响，通过在CIFAR-10上评估3,938个模型变体（9个PyTorch调度族、25种配置），发现最优配置达到86.45% top-1准确率，且调度器选择高度依赖架构，余弦退火和循环学习率策略优于基本衰减策略。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 学习率调度策略对神经网络训练至关重要，但手动选择成本高且难以穷举。现有AutoML方法通常将调度器视为次要超参数，缺乏对其在不同架构下影响的系统性研究。

Method: 从LEMUR神经网络数据集中选取30种代表性架构（卷积和Transformer家族），通过自动化源代码注入，在9个PyTorch调度家族中应用25种调度器配置，在CIFAR-10上评估共计3,938个模型变体。

Result: 最优配置达到86.45% top-1准确率，其中237个变体超过80%准确率；CosineAnnealingWarmRestarts和CyclicLR策略在不同架构上一致优于基本衰减策略；调度器选择与架构类型高度相关。

Conclusion: 调度器选择高度依赖具体架构，不存在通用最优调度器；研究结果构成的准确率景观为原则性调度器选择提供了实用参考。

Original Abstract: Choosing a learning rate scheduling strategy is critical to neural network training, but manual selection is costly and rarely exhaustive. While classical AutoML approaches often treat the scheduler as a secondary hyperparameter, we systematically investigate its impact on classification accuracy across a diverse pool of architectures. We evaluated 30 representative architectures from convolutional and transformer families within the LEMUR neural network dataset. Through automated source-code injection, we applied 25 scheduler configurations across nine PyTorch families, evaluating a total of 3,938 model variants on CIFAR-10. Our best configuration achieved a top-1 accuracy of 86.45%, with 237 variants exceeding 80%. The results show that the choice of scheduler depends heavily on the architecture: CosineAnnealingWarmRestarts and CyclicLR consistently outperform basic decay strategies. The resulting accuracy landscape, contributed to the LEMUR nn-dataset, provides a practical reference for principled scheduler selection.

中文摘要翻译: 选择学习率调度策略对神经网络训练至关重要，但手动选择成本高昂且极少能做到穷举。虽然经典的AutoML方法通常将调度器视为次要超参数，但我们系统地研究了其对多种架构池中分类准确率的影响。我们从LEMUR神经网络数据集中选取了30种来自卷积和Transformer家族的典型架构进行评估。通过自动化源代码注入，我们在9个PyTorch调度家族中应用了25种调度器配置，在CIFAR-10上总共评估了3,938个模型变体。我们的最佳配置达到了86.45%的top-1准确率，其中237个变体准确率超过80%。结果表明，调度器的选择在很大程度上取决于具体架构：CosineAnnealingWarmRestarts和CyclicLR始终优于基本衰减策略。由此产生的准确率景观已贡献至LEMUR nn-dataset，为原则性的调度器选择提供了实用参考。

</details>


### [149] [Stop Guessing When to Stop Testing: Efficient Model Evaluation with Just Enough Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08522)
*Ofir Arviv,Kristjan Greenewald,Yotam Perlitz,Hadar Mulian,Michal Shmueli-Scheuer,Leshem Choshen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种自适应评估框架，将序贯测试与定制化停止准则相结合，解决固定大小基准在模型评估中效率与可靠性之间的矛盾，在Open VLM Leaderboard上实现高达80%的计算成本降低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 固定大小基准的刚性导致其无法适应模型排名、选择、开发测试等不同评估目标对统计功效的差异化需求，造成计算成本过高或可靠性不足的问题。

Method: 将成熟的统计序贯测试范式与定制化的停止准则（如收益递减检测、最小可检测效应量）相结合，构建自适应评估框架，动态管理评估过程中的效率-可靠性权衡。

Result: 在Open VLM Leaderboard上，相比固定大小评估，该框架在保持统计显著性的同时实现了80%的计算成本降低（在2.5分置信区间宽度允许条件下）。

Conclusion: 序贯测试方法能够有效解决固定大小基准的效率瓶颈，为模型评估提供一种原则性的自适应方式，在效率与可靠性之间实现灵活平衡。

Original Abstract: The inherent rigidity of fixed-size benchmarks makes them an inefficient tool for model evaluation. Diverse evaluation objectives, including model ranking, model selection and testing throughout development, demand varying levels of statistical power. The mismatch between fixed sample sizes and these diverse needs results in either excessive computational cost or compromised reliability - a critical concern for model evaluation. To overcome these limitations, we call for adoption of sequential testing in our field. We provide an adaptive evaluation framework, that provides a principled way to navigate the trade-off between efficiency and reliability in model evaluation. Our framework combines the established statistical paradigm of sequential testing with stopping criteria tailored to common evaluation needs such as diminishing returns detection, and minimum detectable effect size. We demonstrate its ability to adaptively manage the efficiency-reliability trade-off on the Open VLM Leaderboard, including, for example, a 80% reduction in computational cost compared to fixed-size evaluation (with a 2.5-point CI width allowance) while maintaining statistical significance.

中文摘要翻译: 固定大小基准固有的刚性使其成为模型评估的低效工具。多样化的评估目标——包括模型排名、模型选择以及开发过程中的测试——对统计功效有着不同水平的需求。固定样本规模与这些多样化需求之间的不匹配，导致要么计算成本过高，要么可靠性受损——这对模型评估而言是一个关键问题。为克服这些局限，我们呼吁在该领域采用序贯测试。我们提供了一个自适应评估框架，该框架提供了一种原则性方式来权衡模型评估中的效率与可靠性。我们的框架将成熟的统计序贯测试范式与针对常见评估需求（如收益递减检测和最小可检测效应量）定制的停止准则相结合。我们在Open VLM Leaderboard上展示了该框架自适应管理效率-可靠性权衡的能力，例如，在保持统计显著性的同时，相比固定大小评估实现了80%的计算成本降低（在2.5分置信区间宽度允许条件下）。

</details>


### [150] [CAAD: Causality-Aware Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detection via Multi-Scale Alignment and Structural Causal Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08555)
*Xin Wang,Yunshi Wen,Yanan He,Haotian Xu,Youlan Zhao,Michel Ferreira Cardia Haddad,Tengfei Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CAAD框架，通过外生变量的格兰杰因果一致性检验实现工业系统异常检测，利用多尺度对齐和梯度矩阵捕捉因果偏差，在真实数据集上优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法仅关注表征的时间相似性，忽略了系统故障和潜在异常中内部因果关系的破坏，因此需要一种能够捕捉因果结构变化的异常检测方法。

Method: CAAD框架将外生时间序列变量建模为残差，将异常识别为外部干预引起的显著偏差；利用多尺度对齐内化系统动力学，并基于梯度矩阵监控内部因果关系的断裂，通过量化动态演化和关系拓扑的因果偏差实现精确检测。

Result: 在真实工业数据集上的大量实验表明，CAAD实现了高精度异常检测，优于大多数最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: CAAD通过持续验证格兰杰因果一致性，能够捕捉微小的因果偏移，为复杂工业系统的异常检测提供了一种有效的新范式。

Original Abstract: The operational integrity of complex industrial systems relies on precise anomaly detection and diagnosis.
